1b1(rgyud): @#//rgya gar skad du/sarba ta thA ga ta tsi t+ta gu h+ya dz+nyA na ar+tha gar+b+ha badzra kro d+ha ku la tan tra pi na thA r+tha bi h+ya yo ga   
1b2(rgyud): sid d+hi nA ma ma hA yA na sU tra//bod skad du/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi thugs gsang ba'i ye shes   
1b3(rgyud): don gyi snying po khro rdo rje'i rigs kun 'dus rig pa'i mdo rnal 'byor grub pa'i rgyud ces bya ba theg pa chen po'i mdo//
1b4(rgyud): bcom ldan 'das dpal sgrol byed thugs rje'i rgyal po la phyag 'tshal lo//'di skad bdag gis thos pa 
1b5(rgyud): dus gcig na/de bzhin gshegs pa tham sa cad kyi bcom ldan 'das dpal kun du bzang po rdo rje sang he  
1b6(rgyud): ru ka gsang ba mchog gi bdag po byin gyis rlabs kyis dbang phyug/mi zad mdzod kyi bkod pa/che ba'i 
1b7(rgyud): don la dam pa'i spyod pas bskyod pa de/rmad du byung ba'i don 'di nyid bshad pa'i gnas gsang ba'i yang 
1b8(rgyud): gsang ba kun gzhi chos kyi dbyings kyi pho brang thig le bdal ba'i krong /ye 'byam rmad du grags pa'i gzhal 
2a1(rgyud): @#//yas rgya phyogs su yongs su ma chad pa/chos nyid kyi nam mkha' las cho 'phrul gyi sprin kun du 'thibs pa/byang chub kyi char mngon par 'bab pa/nyon mongs pa'i gdung ba bangs par gyur pa/ye shes kyi myu 
2a2(rgyud): gu mngon par bskyed pa/mtha' dang dbus med pa/rtse mo kun du rgya mnyam pa/rin po che'i rgyal pos rnam par brgyan pa/mu tig gi lcang lo dang /chun 'chang dang /lnga lngis mdzes pa/nyi ma chen pos gsal btab pa/zla ba 
2a3(rgyud): chen pos phra bkod pa/skar ma dang shin tu skar ma dag gis gzer spras pa/rdo rje rin po che'i 'od snang bas kun du mdangs phyung ba'i mkha' la/rlung nag gi phung po rgya yongs su ma chad par mnyam pa/khrag gi phung po dba' 
2a4(rgyud): klong du 'khrug pa/rgya phyogs su yongs su ma chang pa/ma tang gi gnas dang /bris ma 'bur 'phang bar chags pa/rus pa phung po rgya kun du mnyam pa'i gzhi la 'bar ba'i gzhal yas khang 'od zer kun du 'phro ba/ga bu cung 
2a5(rgyud): brtsegs pa'i rtsig pa la gnam lcags kyi gzer ta le bar btab pa/rtsibs kun du nyi ma dang zla ba rangs pa dang ma rangs par 'phyo ba/nya chen po'i ka gdung la nya phran dral drab gtams pa/zla ba'i ka rten dang nyi ma'i ka lo 
2a6(rgyud): dang /sbrul chun gyi ka rgyan la sogs pa/hU~M gi thog dang /me'i char mngon par 'bab pa/chos nyid kyi bla res rnam par brgyan pa/ye shes kyi lnga lngis mngon par bris pa/rdo rje'i rgyan dang 'phan gyis klubs pa/sbrul 
2a7(rgyud): nag gi zar tshags dang /sprin nag gi grib so lta bus gzhol ba'i bar bar thams cad na rin po che dang /mu tig dang me tog gis mdzes pa/'khor ba'i maN Da la yo bkram la bam ril che chung dang /khog skam stod smad 
2a8(rgyud): dang /yan lag ring thung dang /dbang po'i phreng ba dang /rdo rjes kun du spras pa/sgo dgus kun du gsal bar gyur pa'i gnas na rdo rje rgyal 'tshab zhes bya ba'i zhe sdang rdo rje dang /rdo rje go phur zhes bya ba'i nga rgyal rin chen dang /
2b1(rgyud): rdo rje ging zhes bya ba'i 'dod chags pad mo dang /rdo rje karma zhes bya ba'i phrag dog sna tshogs dang /rdo rje bya rog ces bya ba'i drod kyi rtags 'dzin dang /rdo rje dus mtshams zhes bya ba'i mthun 'jug gi bka' sdod dang /rdo rje spun 
2b2(rgyud): thams cad dang /rdo rje ming thams cad dang /rdo rje rig 'dzin thams cad dang /rdo rje'i lus thams cad dang /rdo rje rnal 'byor thams cad dang /rdo rjes ngag thams cad dang /rdo rjer las thams cad dang /rdo rja yi thams cad dang /
2b3(rgyud): rdo rje ma thams cad dang /rdo rje/gang thams cad dang /rdo rdza mkha' 'gro thams cad dang /rdo rjeshugs kyis 'gro ba thams cad dang /rdo rnga phra men mthams cad dang /rdo rje kun tu 'bar ma thams cad dang /rdorje gnod sbyin thams 
2b4(rgyud): cad dang /rdo rje las srin mo thams cad dang /rdo rje slas dang /byi mo dang /bran mo la sogs pa bud med thams cad dang /rdo rje'am ngag pa la sogs pa'i rdo rje khro bo thams cad 'du bar gyur pa/rdo rje rigs kyi sangs rgyas thams 
2b5(rgyud): cad dang /'gying bag gi tshul gyis bsnyengs pa mi mnga' bar rdo rje'i gdan la 'khor mang po dang thabs gcig tu bzhugs pa ni 'di lta ste/cho 'phrul chen po nam mkha'i dbang phyug ces bya ba'i sangs rgyas dang /cho 'phrul 
2b6(rgyud): chen po rnam grol sbyin pa zhes bya ba'i sangs rgyas dang/cho 'phrul chen po rnam grol sgrogs pa zhes bya ba'i sangs rgya dang /cho 'phrul chen po thams cad grub pa zhes bya ba'i sangs rgyas/nam mkha'i bu mo dbang 
2b7(rgyud): mo zhes bya ba'i rnal 'byor ma chen mo dang /nam mkha'i bu mo dga' sdud ma zhes bya ba'i rnal 'byor chen mo dang /nam mkha' bu mo rnam par gdung ma zhes bya ba'i rnal 'byo ma chen mo dang /nam gza'i bu mo sgron ma 
2b8(rgyud): zhes bya ba'i rnal 'byor mchen mo la sogs te/shin tu gsang ba chen po'i 'khor dang /gzhan yang dam tshig chen po'i 'khor 'di lta ste/dam tshig chen po rdo rje'i snying po zhes bya ba'i sangs rgyas dag/dam tshig chen po 
3a1(rgyud): @#//nor bu'i snying po zhes bya ba'i sangs rgyas dang /dam tshig chen po pad ma'i snying po zhes bya ba'i sad rgyas dang /dam tshig che po bkod pa'i snying po zhes bya ba'i sa da rgyas dang /dam tshig 
3a2(rgyud): zhes bya ba'i rnal 'byor ma chen mo dang /dam tshig chen mo nor bu'i dpang phyug ma zhes bya ba'i rnal 'byor mchen mo dang /dam tshig can mo pad ma'i dbang phyug ma zhes bya ba'i rnam 'byor ma chen mo dang /
3a3(rgyud): dam tshig chen mo rgyan gyi dbang phyug ma zhes bya pa'i rnal 'byor ma chen mo la sogs te/shin tu mi 'da' ba chen po'i 'khor dang /gzhan yang tshad med pa'i 'khor 'di lta ste/gsang ba chen mo byams pa'i snying po zhes bya ba'i 
3a4(rgyud): sangs rgyas dang /gsang ba chen po bde ba'i dbang phyug 
3a5(rgyud): ma zhes bya ba'i rnal 'byoram chen mo dang /gsang ba chen mo 'joms byed dbang phyug ma zhes bya ba' rnal 'byor ma chen mo dadang /gsang ba chen mo btang snyoms kyi snying po zhes bya ba'i sangs rgyas dang /gsang 
3a6(rgyud): ba chen po bde ba'i dbang ba phyug 
3a7(rgyud): ma zhes bya ba'i dbang phyug ma zhes byas ba'i rnal 'byor ma chen mo dang /shin tu tshad med pa'i 'khor dang /gzhan yad rol pa chen po'i 'khor 'di lta ste/rol pa chen po'i sangs rgyas yak+Sha he ru ka zhes bya ba dang /rol pa chen po'i 
3a8(rgyud): sangs rgyas 
3a9(rgyud): rgyas rak+Sha he ru ka zhes bya ba dadang /rol pa chen po'i sadas rgyas b+hig sha he ru ka zhes bya ba da/rol pa chen po'i sadas/rgyas bi shwa he ru ka zhes bya ba da dang /rol pa chen mo'i rnal 'byor ma yak+Sha sho ri ka zhes bya ba dang /ro la pa chen 
3a10(rgyud): mo'i rnal 'byor ma rak+Sha sho ri ka zhes bya ba dadang /ral pa chen mo'i rnal 'byoram b+hig sha sho ri ka zhes bya ba da/rol ba chen mo'i rnal 'byor ma bi shwa sho ri ka zhes bya ba da da/shin tu rol pa chen po'i 'khor dang gzhan yang 
3b1(rgyud): 'jigs byed chen po'i 'khor 'di lta ste/'jigs byed chen po'i 'khod sangs rgyas te sha he ru ka zhes bya ba dang /'jigs byed chen po'i sangs rgyas ke sha he ru ka zhes bya ba ni/'jigs byed chen po'i 
3b2(rgyud): sangs rgyas pa sha he ru ka zhes bya ba/'jigs byed chen po'i sems rgyas bi shwa he ru ka zhes bya ba dang/'jigs byed chen mo'i rnal 'byor ma te sha sho ri ka zhes bya ba dang /'dzigs byed chen mo'i rnal 'byod ma ke sha sho ri ka zhes bya ba/'jigs
3b3(rgyud): bya ba dang/'jigs byed pa'i sangs rgyas bi shwa he ru ga zhes bya ba dang /'jig byed chen mo'i rnal 'byoram bi sha sho ri ka zhes bya bal sogs pa shin du 'jigs byed chen po'i 'kho dang /gzhan yang 'joms byed chen po'i 'khor
3b4(rgyud): 'di lta ste/'joms byed chen po'i sangs rgya sa trag sha te ru ka zhes bya ba dang 'joms byed chen mo'i sangs rgyas hri sha he ru ka zhes bya ba yang /'joms byed chen po'i
3b5(rgyud): sangs rgyas ka sha he ru ka zhes bya ba:dang /'joms byed chen mo'i rnal 'byo ra ma trag sha sho ri ka zhes bya ba:dang /'dzoms byed chen mo'i rnal 'phyor ma hri sha sho ri ka zhes bya dang 'joms byed chenamo'i rnal 'byor ma ka sha
3b6(rgyud): sho ri ka zhes bya ba dang/'joms byed chen mo'irnal 'byor ma bag sha sho ri ka la sogs te/shin du 'joms byed chen po'i mkhor dang /gzhan yang za byed chen po'i 'khor 'di lta ste/za byed chen po'i sangs rgyas ru ti he ru ka zhes
3b7(rgyud): bya ba dang/za byed chen po'i sangasargyas rag ti he ru ka zhes bya ba dang/za byed chen pa'i sangs rgyas kha hi he ru ka zhes bya ba da/za byed chen po'i sangs
3b8(rgyud): ri ka zhes bya ba dang/za byad chen po'i rnal 'byor ma rtag ti sho ri ka zhes bya ba dang /za byed chen mo'i rnal 'byor ma kha hi sho ri ka zhes bya ba da/za byad chen mo'i rnal 'byoram sa ha sho ri ka zhes bya ba la sogs pa rnal
4a1(rgyud): @#//'byor ma'i 'khor rab tu mang zhing bsam gyis mi khyab pa'i he ru ka mtha' yas pa sha stag rnams dang /gzhan yang gsang ba'i bu mo'i 'khor 'di lta ste/gsang ba'i bu mo dkar mo zhes bya ba dang /gsang ba'i bu mo 
4a2(rgyud): rkun mo zhes bya ba dang /gsar ba'i bu mo rmongs mo zhes bya ba dang /gsad ba'i bu mo ro langs ma zhes bya ba dang /gsang pa'i bu mo lcos mo zhes bya ba dang //gsang ba'i bu mo gtum mo zhes bya ba dang /gsung ba'i bu mo sma sha 
4a3(rgyud): can zhes bya ba dang /gsang nga'i bu mo ma tshogs ma zhes bya ba la sogs pa shin tu gsang ba'i bu mo'i 'khor bsam gyis mi khyab pa dang /gzhan yang dga' ba'i bu mo'i 'khor 'di la lta ste/dga' ba'i bu mo seng gdong ma zhes bya 
4a4(rgyud): ba dang /dga' ba'i bu mo bya rgod gdong ma zhes bya ba dang /dga' ba'i bu ma stag gdong ma zhes bya ba dang/dga' ba'i bu mo wa gdong ma zhes bya ba dang dga' ba'i bu mo khyi gdong ma zhes bya ba dangdga' ba'i bu mo dur bya 
4a5(rgyud): gdong ma zhes bya ba dang /dga' ba'i bu mo khwa gdong ma zhes bya ba dang dga' ba'i bu mo mun gdong ma zhes bya ba la sogs ba shin tu dga' ba'i bu mo'i 'khor bsamagyis mi khyab pa dang //gzhan yang shin tu gus pa'i bu mo'i 
4a6(rgyud): 'khor 'di lta ste/shin tu gus pa'i bu mo rta gdong ma zhes bya ba dangs/shin tu gus pa'i bu ma phag gdong ma zhes bya ba dang shin tu gus pa'i bu mo spre'u gdong ma zhes bya ba dnga'/shin tu gus pa'i bu mothal byed ma la sogs pa 
4a7(rgyud): shin tu gus pa'i bu mo'i 'khor dang /gzhan yang shin tu gsod pa chen po'i sras kyi 'khor la 'di lta ste/shin tu gsod pa chen po dpal stag gi mgrin zhes bya ba dang /shin tu gsod pa chen pa dpal g.yag gi mgrin zhes 
4a8(rgyud): bya ba dang /shin tu gsong pa chen po dpal sha ba'i mgrin zhes bya ba dang/shin tu gsod pa chen mo dpal gzig gi mgrin zhes bya ba dag/shin tu gsod pa chen bo dpal rta'i mgrin zhes bya ba dangshin tu gsod pa chen 
4b1(rgyud): dpal spyang ku'i mgrin zhes bya ba dang /shin du gsod pa chen po dpal seng ge mgrin zhes bya ba dang/shin du gsod pa chen pa dpal dom gyi mgrin zhes bya ba dang/shin du gsod pa chen po pal phag gi mgrin zhes bya ba 
4b2(rgyud): dang/shin du gsod pa che po dpal dred mo'i mgrin zhes bya ba dang//shin du gsod ma chen mo rdo rje gsod ma zhes bya ba dang/shin dugasod ma chen mo rdo rje dur khrod ces bya ba dang/shin du gsad ma chen mo rdo rje gtun khung zhes bya 
4b3(rgyud): ba dang /shin du gsod ma chen mo rde rdzogatum mo zhes bya ba dang/shin du bsod ma chen mo rdo rje mda' snyems ma zhes bya ba dang/shin tu gsod ma chen mo rdo rje sgra 'byin ma zhes bya ba dang/shin gyi gsod ma chen mo rdo rje za byed ma zhes bya 
4b4(rgyud): ba dang/shin du gsod ma chen ma zhes bya ba dang//shin tu gsod ma chen ma chen mo rdo rje spor ma zhes bya ba dang/shin dang gsod ma chen mo'i 'khor rab tu mang po rnams dang/de dag la sogs te rdul phra rab snyed dang thabs 
4b5(rgyud): gcig go/yum rdo rje kun du bzang mos nga sho rigs kyang nyid kyi sku la rnam par 'khril te/mdun gyis bltas nas thabs gcig go/de nas bcom ldan 'das gsang ba mchog gi bdag po des chos thams cad dgongs pa 
4b6(rgyud): mnyam pa nyid la shar bar mkhyen nas sku mnyam pa nyid mi dmigs pa zhes bya ba'i ngang la snyoms par zhugs so//gsung mnyam pa nyid mi dmigs zhes bya ba'i ngang la rnam par zhugs so//thugs mnyam pa nyid mi 
4b7(rgyud): dmigs pa zhes bya ba'i dang la rnam par bzhugs te/'jug pa 'am/ldog pa 'am/rnam par ldog pa mi dmigs so//de nas bcom ldan 'dasagasang ba mchog gi bdag pa des dbyings kyi ngang la mi g.yo bar rnal 
4b8(rgyud): bzhin du yum rdo rje kun du bzad mos stong pa nyid kyi glu 'di blangs te rnam par bskul to//yod med stong pa shin du rnal 'byor mthus//nam mkha' bzhin las dam tshig nye bar gshegs//ye shes rgya mtsho rnam par 
5a1(rgyud): @#//grol ba'i klong //mi gnas mi dmigs nam mkha'i dbyings nas bzhengs zhes bskul nas/bcom ldan 'das gsang ba'i mchog gi bdag po des chos thams cad byang chub sems la shar bar 
5a2(rgyud): mkhyen nas stong pa nyid kyi ngang las rdo rje'i sku mngon du phyung ste/dper na nam mkha' dang pa las zla ba dri ma med par shar ba lta bu'o//de nas yum sa nga sho ri ka la gzigs te/'dzum pa rnam par mdzad do//dper na nyi 
5a3(rgyud): ma stong las 'od kyi zer bkye ba lta bu'o//de nas 'od kyi zer de dag las byang chub kyi sprin mngon par 'khrigs te/rol pa'i char mngon par phab pas rol pa sna tshogs kyi 'brug dang /rol pa sna tshogs kyi glog dang /rol pa sna 
5a4(rgyud): tshogs kyi mchod pa dang /rol pa sna tshogs gyi glu dang /rol pa sna tshogs kye gar dang /rol pa sna tshogs ki rnga dang /dung dang /ker kor dang /gling pu dang rol pa sna tshogs kyi rgyan dang /rol pa sna tshogs 
5a5(rgyud): kyi sgeg pa dang /steg pa dang 'khyud pa dang /steng ba dang sbyor ba dang /bstim pa dang mnyes pa dang /rol pa sna tshog sa kyis gsol ba dang /myang ba dang 'cha' ba dang /bde ba dang spro ba dang /'dze ba dang 'jug pa dang /rol ba sna tshogs kyi dga' ba chen po 
5a6(rgyud): la 'jug pa rnam par mdzad do//de nas yum kun du bzang mo des spyan zim bag gis rnam par 'dud pa'i tshul du bcom ldan 'das gsang ba'i mchog gi bdag po la rnam par bltas te/rnal 'byor sbyor ba'i phyag lus 
5a7(rgyud): kun du sa la brdabs nas rol pa chen po'i glu 'dis gsol ba btab po//tshogs chen gnyis rdzogs thugs dam dbyings kyi lha//longs spyod rdzogs mdzad rol pa chen po'i sku//rdo rje sems pa skal bzangs dpal khyod bdag/
5a8(rgyud): thabs kyi rol pa sa bde chen dbyings nas bzhengs//de nas bcom ldan 'das gsang pa mchog gi bdag po nges glu des bskul bar thugs su chud nas/bde ba chen po'i ngang de las cho 'phrul chen por rnam par bzhengs te/
5b1(rgyud): rnam par 'gyid pa dang/rnam par brjid pa dang/gar gyi phyag rgya chen po dang/rnam par bshad pa sna tshogs dang/zhabs kyi brkyang bskum dang/sku'i 'gyur bag dang/smin ris kyi 'bri ku ta bsgyur ba dang/sprul pa'i 'od zer 
5b2(rgyud): gyis nam mkha' thams cad kun du khyab par mdzad do//de nas thams cad dang/thams cad la mtshan ma dang/rnam pa dang yod pa dang/med pa thams cad la gsang ba mchog gi bdag po des kun la khyab par mdzad de/de 
5b3(rgyud): dag gi rang bzhin du rnam par gnas so//de nas rdo rje kun du bzang mo de ngo mtshar du gyur te/yang rnal 'byor pa'i phyag lus kun du sa la brdabs te cho 'phrul 'byung ba'i glu 'dis gsol ba btab po//ye shes sgyu mas rnam 
5b4(rgyud): par rol pa'i sku//thabs dang shes rab che la mnga' brnyes pa'i//'phags rje sprul pa mang po 'byung ba'i gnas//bde gshegs bde bar gshegs mdzad bzhengs su gsol//de nas bcom ldan 'das gsang ba mchog gi 
5b5(rgyud): bdag po des rdo rje rol pa'i de kho na nyid ces bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs nas cad mi gsung bar bzhugs so//de nas yum rdo rje kun du bzang mo dang/dam tshig chen mo rdo rje sems dpa' la sogs pa 'khor shin du 
5b6(rgyud): mang po rnams ngo mtshar du gyur te/de dag thams cad mgrin gcig tu rdo rje rol pa de kho na'i glu 'dis bskul te gsol ba btab bo//e ma'o/yid bzhin sku mchog nam mkha' lta bu las//khyab pa'i byin rlabs rdo rje gar mdzad 
5b7(rgyud): cing/gzi brjid sprin dpung rol pas bsgyings pa'i sku//thogs med rdo rje rol pa'i cho 'phrul ston//e ma'o/yid bzhin gsung mchog sgra snyan lta bu las//snyan pa'i byin rlabs rdo rje brjod mdzad cing//yan lag rgya mtsho mi 
5b8(rgyud): zad bkod pa'i gsung//seng ge sgra yis gsang sngags rgya mtsho ston//e ma'o/ye shes snying po nyi ma lta bu las//ye shes byin brlabs rdo rje bde mdzad cing//thig le spros bral rnam par mi rtog thugs//rnam grol de nyid des 
6a1(rgyud): pa'i cho 'phrul ston//mi shes blun rmongs 'dam bying 'gro ba rnams//sdug bsngal rgya mtshos bcings pa thabs kyis khrol//ma rig mun pas ldongs pa don gyi phye//'khor ba'i rgya mtsho rol pa'i gru yis sgrol//
6a2(rgyud): de bzhin gshegs pa kun gyi thugs chen po//ye shes don gyi snying po mchog dang ldan//rnal 'byor theg chen rdo rje kun 'dus rgyud//'dus pa'i tshogs la rgya cher bshad du gsol//a la la ho/zhes nges par btud de 
6a3(rgyud): gsol ba btab po//de nas bcom ldan 'das gsang ba mchog gi bdag po des rdo rje rol pa de kho na nyid kyi glu des gsol ba btab pa mkhyen nas/'khor thams cad la rnam par gzigs te/'dzum pa mdzad do//
6a4(rgyud): 'dzum pa mdzad pas thugs rnam par dag pa'i glod nas rig pa'i nyi ma brjod kyis mi lang ba'i yang mi lang ba'i 'od zer dang bcas pa 'byam klas pa khyad par shar bar mdzad do//'od zer de dag re re las rdo rje he ru ka rab tu 
6a5(rgyud): mang ba'i tshogs bshad par mdzad do//de dag re re las kyang sangs rgyas 'bum phrag 'khor so so'i zhabs 'bring bcas pa lhun gyis grub par mdzad do//de dag las rig pa'i nyi ma'i char mngon par phab pas mun pa'i klong 
6a6(rgyud): thams cad bsal bar mdzad do//rig pa'i chos kyi 'brug sgra dang /rig pa'i chos kyi char dang /rig pa'i chos kyis glu sna tshogs dang /gar sna tshogs dang /rig pa'i chos kyi mchod pa sna tshogs byung ste/'byam klas pa'i 
6a7(rgyud): nam mkha' la sgra sna tshogs phan tshun dang /yan man dang /sne'u zhi la sogs pa thams cad du khyab par ling long phyad par byung ngo //mchod pa'i char yang mngon par phab/rmad du byung ba'i sku bsam gyis mi khyab 
6a8(rgyud): pa yang ling long phyang rmad du byung ba'i thugs kyang lhang nge'o//rmad du byung ba'i gsung bsam gyis mi khyab pa yang a la la ho zhes sgra sgrogs/sangs rgyas 'bum phrag kyang gsung dbyangs bsam gyis mi khyab pa'i 
6b1(rgyud): sgra gcig tu/e ma'o rmad byung rol pa'i chos kyi don byung ngo zhes sgrogs/'khor 'bum phrag dang gdul bya sna tshogs spro ba'i ngo mtshar skyed/rig pa'i rtsal sbyangs don gyi phye ba phyung //dam zhing ngo mtshar bar gyur te//thams 
6b2(rgyud): cad nye bar gnas so//de nas yang bcom ldan 'das gsang ba mchog gi bdag po des 'khor rnam par sbyong ba'i rdo rje rgyan zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs so//snyoms par zhugs nas rdo rje sngags kyi tshig 
6b3(rgyud): 'di gsungs so//hU~M hU~M hU~M rdo rje 'du bar bsdus pa yi//dam tshig chen po'i rig 'dzin rnams//bla 'og rim pa ji bzhin du//rdo rje gsang ba'i gral 'khod de//rigs ma yin pa rigs min 'gyur//bskal pa bzang po'i dam tshig 
6b4(rgyud): 'di//skal ba ngan la 'bab mi 'gyur//de las 'das na de brlag byed//hU~M kro d+ha dz+wa la sa ma ya/rak+Sha dz+wa la sa ma ya/yak+Sha dz+wa las ma ya/tsi ta ta dz+wa la sa ma ya zhes gsungs pa tsam gyis zhal ljags kyi dbang po 'od me dpung 
6b5(rgyud): chen po las khro bo 'od kyi phreng ba 'bar ba dang /srin mo 'od kyi phreng ba 'bar pa dang /gnod sbyin 'od kyi phreng ba 'khrug pa dang /za byed 'od kyi phreng ba 'bar ba'i brgyud mar byung bas ru tra can dang /bdud dang srin mo dang /gnod sbyin 
6b6(rgyud): dang srin po dang /'jug sred dang /sred med dang dbang phyug dang /stobs ldan dang spre'u dang /ra dang lu 'di dang /sbrang ma dang bya rog dang /me khyer dang 'khor ba dang /chu dang sa dang /nam mkha' dang me dang /sgra dang bsam ngan la sogs 
6b7(rgyud): pa brlag ste/mi gnas par gyur cing rmad du byung ba nyid du mdzad do//de nas 'od zer de dag mkha' la 'khyil ba dang /'khor mang po dag shin du skrag ste gsol btab pa/e ma'o//e ma bdag cag bcom ldan 'das kyis byin 
6b8(rgyud): gyis brlab du gsol zhes gsol ba btab pa las/bcom ldan 'das gsang ba mchog gi bdag po des zhal gyi mdangs phyung ste/'od zer de dag ye shes kyi 'od zhi zhing 'khyil ba dang /mdzes shing bde ba dang ldan pas thams 
7a1(rgyud): @#//
7b1(rgyud): shin du bla kyal ba'i spobs pa can du gyur to//byin gyis brlabs nas kun du mi gzob rab tu bag dang ldan pa chen por gyur to//de nas sems dpa' rdo rje des las kyi mtshan ma de lag tu blangs te/stabs sna tshogs 
7b2(rgyud): dang /gar dang /klu sna tshogs rdo rje tshig tu len cing rdo rje rgya gram las kyi 'khor lo de lan rnam pa bcu gnyis su skor nas grangs gnyis dang lnga bsgyur bas zhing khams mtha' dag rnam par g.yos te/g.yos rab tu g.yos 
7b3(rgyud): kun du rab tu g.yos/'gul rab tu 'gul/kun du rab tu 'gul/ldeg rab tu ldeg/kun du rab tu ldeg/'khrugs rab tu 'khrugs/kun du rab tu 'khrugs/'ur rab tu 'ur/kun du rab tu 'ur/chem rab tu chem 
7b4(rgyud): kun du rab tu chem par gyur te/zhing khams thams cad ni gsang ba'i dkyil 'khor sha stag tu gyur/bcud yul thams cad ni phyag rgya chen por gyur/rnam par rngam pa'i tshul gyis ston pa'i mdun logs su seng 
7b5(rgyud): ge rdo rjes mtshan pa'i gdan la 'gying bag gi tshul gyis rnam par gnas so//de nas 'dus pa'i 'khor tshogs phal mo che rnams kyis bcom ldan 'das la 'di skad ces gsol to//e ma bcom ldan 'das sems 
7b6(rgyud): dpa' rdo rje 'di ni 'di ltar mthu che ba 'di ltar byin rlabs che ba/'di ltar rgya che ba/'di ltar mkhas pa/'di ltar gsal zhing zab pa ni bcom ldan 'das kyi thugs las skyes pa legs so zhes gsol ba dang /bcom 
7b7(rgyud): ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/'dus pa chen po'i tshogs sems dpa' rdo rje 'di ni byang chub kyi sems thams cad kyi phyi mo/snying rje thams cad kyi bdag po/sangs rgyas thams cad kyi rje btsun byang chub 
7b8(rgyud): sems dpa' rig pa 'drin pa thams cad kyi yab/rnal 'byor pa thams cad kyi mes chen po/mya ngan las 'das pa thams cad kyi ded dpon/bdud thams cad kyi yang bdud chen po/chos thams cad kyi mdzod 
8a1(rgyud): @#//ye shes thams cad kyi 'byung gnas chen por gyur pa yin no//de ci'i phyir zhe na/thog ma dang tha ma med pa mnyam pa'i dus/pha mtha' las kyang pha mtha'/dpag tu med cing bsam du med pa/'byaM 
8a2(rgyud): klas pa'i dus nas bya ba byas shing byed pa byas pa/mngon par rdzogs par sangs rgyas pa yin te/chos thams cad kyi rang bzhin du sbyor zhing gnas pas na/rgyal ba thams cad kyi thugs las skyes shing thams cad kyi yab du gyur 
8a3(rgyud): pa yin te/de 'dra'o zhes bka' stsal pa las 'dus pa'i 'khor thams cad ngo mtshar du gyur te/bcom ldan 'das 'di ni 'di ltar ngo mtshar lags so//bde bar gshegs pa 'di ngo mtshar lags so//sems dpa' rdo rje 
8a4(rgyud): dam pa la logs su btud de lus sa la brdabs pa'i phyag rnal 'byor lugs su byas te rmad du byung ba'i me tog dang /tshogs kyi mchod pa sna tshogs byas te/'di ltar rmad byung ngo mtshar khyod//sangs rgyas thugs las 
8a5(rgyud): rgyal skyes pa//ci ltar zhing 'dir snang zhig gu//rnal 'byor yab gyur rgyal ba'i sras//ngo mtshar khyod kyis spyod pas na//bka' yi 'khor lo rab bskor ba//bsgrub pa'i lam mchog dam pa dag/tshig gis mtshon de 
8a6(rgyud): gsal bar mdzod//a la la ho zhes gsol ba btab bo//de nas dam pa rdo rjes rdo rje bskor te bsgyur nas brjid bag tu mtshon te smras pa/oM A hU~M/a la la ho/rgyal ba'i sras pos cang mi gsung /mi gsung bas ni rgya cher 
8a7(rgyud): bshad/bshad du med pa'i chos 'di ni/rgyal ba rnams kyis ma bshad de//de ltar ston pa rgyal mchog kyang //rnam par brjod par mi bzhed de//rgya cher bshad par dgongs pa'o//skye ba med pa'i chos rnams ni//ma skyes par 
8a8(rgyud): yang snying por bzhed//snying po'i don du gang gyur pa//tshig tu smra ba legs ma yin//spros pa nyid na gol ba zhes//rgyal ba rnams kyis yang dag bshad//de bas rdo rje rgyal po nyid//bka' ma stsal bar dgongs pa'o//dgongs 
8b1(rgyud): pa'i don rnams gang 'byin pa//ji ltar me long gzugs brnyan ltar//dgongs don rig la snang gyur pa//ma skyes snying po'i don gyis na//tshig tu spro bar ma yin na'ang //mtshan ma tshig gi rnal 'byor pa//tshig la zhib 
8b2(rgyud): cing don la rtsing //tshig phyar blo 'bring rtsal zhen rnams//bkri phyir bsam pa rangs bya ba//mi dmigs pa las dmigs pa yi//cho 'phrul tshig gis mtshon pa ni//bder gshegs rgyal ba kun gyi thugs//gsang ba de nyid snying 
8b3(rgyud): po'i don//mnyam nyid byang chub theg chen po//gsal bar bkod de nges brjod kyis//rgya chen 'dus pa'i tshogs rnams nyon//nga ni dus gsum bzhugs pa yi//rgyal ba kun gyi snying po ste//stong par sbas pa'i don 'di la//
8b4(rgyud): dpes mtshon tshig gi lde mig gis//mnyam nyid rig pa'i sgo dbye byas//sbas pa'i don 'byin gsal byed pa//sa lu phub khrod snang me bzhin//bsal bteg snang rnyed sa ler 'dug/zab mor spyod pa la mos pas//gang zhig don 
8b5(rgyud): la 'dris pa yi//gsang sngags spyod pa'i rang bzhin ni//'thun pa'i dmigs bcu dper bzhag ste//rnal 'byor can gyis shes par bya//sgyu ma smig sgyu rmi lam dang //gzugs brnyan dri za grong khyer dang //brag cha chu zla mig yor 
8b6(rgyud): dang //chu bur 'khrul dang mgal mer snang //sgrub dang gsang sngags spyod pa dang //grub pa dang ni gnas dag dang //bzlas pa'i sgra dang phyag rgyar rtog/ting 'dzin lta ba cho 'phrul brtag/ji ltar sgyu ma'i mtshan nyid ni//skyes bu dag 
8b7(rgyud): gi rtsol ba dang //sman dang sngags kyi sbyor ba las//dbang po yid 'phrog cir snang yang //de la 'gro 'ong ga la yod//de ltar sgrub pa'i mtshan nyid kyang //yig 'bru rdzas kyi sbyor ba las//grub par snang yang rang bzhin med//
8b8(rgyud): ji ltar smig rgyu'i mtshan nyid ni//zhing pa'i dus kyi zla ba la//drod dang nyi ma'i sbyor ba las//byis pa gdungs pa'i rang bzhin gyis//chu yi 'du shes ci bskyed kyang //tshim pa'i rgyud ni ga la 'gyur//gsang sngags ming gi khyad par 
9a1(rgyud): @#//yang /rang bzhin mi dmigs stong pa la//mtshan ma'i tha snyad ming du btags//de nyid stong pas ming ming gis //dper na rmi lam rang bzhin ni//skyes bu gnyid kyi dbang gyur na//thang cig skad cig yud tsam dang 
9a2(rgyud): lo la sogs pa de bzhin du//bde dang sdug bsngal de bzhin myong //sangs nas ci yang mi dmigs ltar//de bzhin sngags kyi spyod pa dag/stong pa nyid kyi rang bzhin la//spyod pa'i rnam pa cir snang yang //brtags na dmigs 
9a3(rgyud): pa kun dang bral//gzugs brnyan snang ba'i mtshan nyid ni//dper na gdong dang me long las//brten nas bzhin gyi gzugs byung yang //reg par gyur pa ga la yod//de bzhin sngags kyi dngos grub kyang //sgrub dang ting 
9a4(rgyud): 'dzin yo byad las//brten nas dngos grub tshul byung yang //gsol thob dmigs pa ga la yod//dri za gnas pa'i rang bzhin ni//mkha' 'am yid kyi mkha' ngos la//dri za'i gzugs ni gar byung yang //grong khyer de yang 
9a5(rgyud): de nas byung //byung nas de yang mi rtag ltar//gsang sngags grub pa'i gnas dag kyang //chos sam yid kyi mkha' ngos la/rnal 'byor gsang bdag gar bskyed par//de nyid gnas pa'i gzhal med khang //byung yang de 
9a6(rgyud): la gnas mi 'gyur//brag cha grag pa'i mtshan ma ni//dper na ngos la sgras rkyen pas//ci bzhin sgra ltar brag cha byung //byung yang sgrar thos dmigs pa med//de bzhin sngags tshig bzlas pa'i sgra//dper na med las yod 
9a7(rgyud): brtags pa'i//mtshan ma la brten stong pa'i sgra//brjod pa tsam du bstan par zad//ji ltar chu yi zla ba ni//sgrib med mkha' las zla shar ba//gzugs brnyan dang ba'i chu nang snang //snang yang gzung ba'i rtog med pa//de 
9a8(rgyud): bzhin gzugs snang phyag rgya yang //sems kyi mkha' las skur gsal ba//phyag rgya rig 'dzin ngo la snang //de ltar snang yang 'dzin rtog bral//ji ltar char gyi rgyun bzhin du//chu las chu bur rnam par 'byung //de yang gcig 
9b1(rgyud): gi rnam pa las//mang po'i rnam grangs du ma byung //sdud cing phyir yang dum bur 'chad//de bzhin rnal 'byor sgrub pa yi//ting 'dzin rnam pa mang por 'dod//'khrul pa snang ba'i rang bzhin ni//spros bral dngos po stong pa 
9b2(rgyud): las//srog dang shed can byed pa po//sems can pa la sogs par snang //mig nad ngo la rdzas ltar snang //'khrul pa kho na snang bar zad//de bzhin sngags kyi lta ba yang //cho 'phrul mang po cir 'phrul ba//snang ba med pa'i 
9b3(rgyud): rang bzhin la//'khrul pa sna tshogs snang bar zad//rnal 'byor can gyis de ltar lta//ji ltar mgal me'i rang bzhin ni//gang dag lag na mgal me thogs//bskor na 'khor lo'i rgyun ltar snang //de la 'khor lo'i rang 
9b4(rgyud): bzhin med//de bzhin sngags kyi cho 'phrul yang //mi mnyam mnyam pa'i rang bzhin las//ji ltar dmigs pa'i don 'byung ste//cho 'phrul sna tshogs rgyun mi 'chad//de phyir mtshan ma'i chos thams cad//mtshan 
9b5(rgyud): med ngang du gcig pas na//rtog pa'i bag chags ci 'byung yang //chos kun dbyings su gcig pa'i phyir//rtog 'jug spros pa'i mtshan ma kun//ma byung ma skyes dbyings nyid la//g.yos pa med cing cho 'phrul 
9b6(rgyud): 'byung //yon tan sna tshogs cher 'byung ba'i//gter gyi dbyig 'di gang mthong ba//sangs rgyas kun gyi yon tan yang //skad cig gcig la de la 'dus//de bas yid bzhin rin chen 'di//ldog pa med pas dang du 
9b7(rgyud): blang //don dam yang dag gter gyi mchog/rnal 'byor kun gyi nor mchog ste//gang rtogs de 'thob gcig pu nyid//'brel med gzhan gyi thob par dka'//bdag med gnyis bral bde chen klong //sangs rgyas kun 
9b8(rgyud): gyi bde ba che//ye nas lhun grub 'dus ma byas//thig le chos nyid ye 'byam pas//kun khyab bdal bas snying por 'dus//mi dmigs spros bral byang chub sems//zab mor snang ba'i nges don 'di//mkha' la bdud 
10a1(rgyud): @#//'drar gting dpag dka'//rnal 'byor dmigs ldan blo chung zhing //gang zhig rtsal zhan de rtog pa//g.yor mo'i tshul du bdag brtags pa//brtags kyang rtogs par mi 'gyur ro//dper na nyi ma'i snying po la//mig 
10a2(rgyud): gi dbang pos rab brtags na//de brtags don de mi rtogs kyi//'od zer gyis ni mig rdzis 'gyur//de 'drar byis pa'i rang bzhin gyis//zab mo'i don la cir brtags kyang //yang dag don de mi mthong gi/blo zhan byis pa ga 
10a3(rgyud): chad rgyal//brtag tu med pa'i chos rnams ni//rtog pa can gyis mi rtog te//gang zhig de don mi rtogs phyir//g.yang sar ltung ba ci cha yod//gdod nas ma thos shin du dge/bsam dang bral bas de bzhin gzhag/mi brtags 
10a4(rgyud): bzhag pa'i chos 'di gang //byis pa rnams la ngas ma bstan//bstan kyang go bar mi 'gyur te//bstan du med cing go bar dka'//tshig gis brjod pa kun dang bral//'di zhes bstan du ga la yod//med pas bstan pa'i 
10a5(rgyud): dmigs mi phyed//mi dmigs chos la blo sbyangs shing //yid kyi me long gsal byas na//nam mkha' dag la rab brtags na//snang ba med pa'i rang bzhin ni//nam mkha' lta bur de nyid 'gyur//chos nyid nam mkha' 
10a6(rgyud): lta bu ste//nam mkha' la yang mtshan nyid med//nam mkha' gang ming brjod byed pa//brjod pa de nyid dngos po ste//dngos po de yi ming brjod kyang //nam mkha' de la 'gag mi 'gyur//de bzhin mi rtog rjod byed pa//brjod pa 
10a7(rgyud): de nyid rnam rtog ste//rnam rtog de yi ming brjod kyang /mi rtog de la 'gag mi 'gyur/de 'drar sems kyang de ltar blta//sems nyid gzhan du ma grub ste//de bzhin chos kyang grub ma yin//sems dang chos nyid gnyis 
10a8(rgyud): med de//sems nyid gang yin chos nyid de//chos nyid kyang ni de bzhin no//chos sems gnyis med ldan gyur pa//bla med byang chub chen por bshad//sems ni nam mkha' lta bu ste//rnam par rtog bral gang mi rtogs//
10b1(rgyud): de bzhin sa gzhi chen po ltar//bla med byang chub myu gu skyed//me yi rang bzhin ltar snang bas//rmongs pa'i bud shing sreg par byed//rlung gi rang bzhin yin pas na//mi shes pa yi rdul kun sel//chu yi rang bzhin yin pas 
10b2(rgyud): na//nyon mongs gdung ba kun sim byed//sman gyi rang bzhin yin pas na//'khor ba'i gcong rnying rnam par 'byin//de ltar rtsi'i rang bzhin pas//'tsho ba rnams kyis gsos su 'bab//de bzhin rno dpal rang bzhin pas//sdug 
10b3(rgyud): bsngal rgya mtsho rnam par gcod//nyi ma lta bur snang ba yis//mun khang snang ston sel bar byed//ri rab lta bu'i rang bzhin pas//brtan po mi 'gyur g.yo ba med//rgya mtsho lta bu'i rang bzhin pas//mi g.yo ba la byin rlabs 
10b4(rgyud): che//sems ni 'di ltar cho 'phrul pas//de phyir 'khor dang mya ngan 'das//gnyis su snang ba ma yin te//sems kyi rang bzhin bzang ngan las//gnyis kyi 'du shes phye ste bstan//sems las yang dag ye shes 'byung //
10b5(rgyud): de bzhin bde dang sdug bsngal 'byung //chos dang sangs rgyas ye shes 'byung //dge 'dun yang ni de bzhin no//'di las pha rol phyin tshogs dang //byang chub dang ni pad mo 'byung //'di las rdo rje sems dpa' dang //dri med 
10b6(rgyud): rin chen dpa' byin 'byung //de bzhin rdo rje dpag med dang //sna tshogs rgyal ba 'di las byung //'di las phyag rgya sna tshogs dang //rig pa'i snying po thams cad 'byung //'di las 'dod chags zhe sdang dang //sdug 
10b7(rgyud): bsngal nga rgyal phrag dog 'byung //gti mug dag kyang de bzhin te//'di las khams gsum thams cad dang //skyes pa bud med ma ning 'byung //'di las 'dodad dang 'dod gzir dang //nyon mongs gdung 'dod chags pa 
10b8(rgyud): 'byung //ri dang skye bo de bzhin du//nam mkha' dang ni rlung dag dang //sred pa'i me ni rab tu 'byung //'di las sa dang chu dang ni//rdo dang sa bon myu gu 'byung //de bzhin glog dang skar ma dang //nyi ma zla ba sprin rnams 'byung //
11a1(rgyud): @#//
11b1(rgyud): dang tshul khrims so sor snang //byis pa rnams ni tha tshig sdom//bden gnyis rgyu la ston par grags//bla med mchog la dam tshig spyod//gcig gi don las ma g.yos kyang //'dul dbang do la so sor grags//kho na nyid ldan

11b7(rgyud): med pa'i chos nyid la//tshig 'dzin nor pa'i rtsol byar med//bgrod du med pa'i chos nyid la/bgrod pa'i dkyil 'khor ming dang bral//bsam du med pa'i chos nyid la//rtog sems bsam pa'i dmigs dang bral//brtsal med 
11b8(rgyud): pa yi chos nyid la//brtsal med ye nas lhun gyis grub//mtha' dbus med pas brdal ba che//phyi nang med pas yo gsal khyab//skyon ye na med par lhun gyis rdzogs//bsgrub du med pas brtsal sems bral//gzung du med 
12a1(rgyud): @#//pas dmigs dang bral//thams cad mkhyen pas byin rlabs che//gdod nas byang chub snying por gnas//spyod pa'i khyad par 'di lta bu//des na gcig las med bzhin du//spyod 'thun rtsol ba'i rang bzhin la//rnam 
12a2(rgyud): rtog spyod pa so sor snang //'khrul pa thams cad 'khor bar spyod//rgyu 'bras mi slu bden don spyod//mi dmigs pa la nam mkha' spyod//de ltar spyod pa kun spyod kyang //dam pa'i don de spyod mchog nyid//dam par gyur pa 
12a3(rgyud): thams cad la//dam pa'i spyod pa de las med//skyes bu mchog gis de skad bshad//the tsom med par shes par gyis//ma bcos byang chub de bzhin nyid//bsgom du med pa'i chos nyid la//bsgom pa dmigs su med pa 
12a4(rgyud): ste//ting 'dzin zhes ni gdags su med//bsgoms kyang rnyed pa ma yin la//de bzhin ma bsgoms stor ba med//bsgoms chos sna tshogs cir snang yang //bsgoms nas de yi ngang mi gnas//de bas byis pa'i spyod yul yin//
12a5(rgyud): dper na byis pa'i rang bzhin gyis//sa la sogs pa gzugs sna tshogs//mdzes par byas nas skad cig mthong //mthong ba nyid na ma grub ste//skad cig kho nas zhig gyur nas//sa la sogs pa'i rdul du snang //de bzhin rnal 
12a6(rgyud): 'byor byis pa yis//gzugs la sogs pa sna tshogs bsgom//sems kyi bye brag skad cig la//yid kyi rgyu ba sna tshogs 'byung //byung ba bzhin du de ma grub//sems kyi skad cig dran pa la//yid kyi dmigs pa rgyun chad nas//
12a7(rgyud): byung tshor chad pa'i mtha' spangs te//chos rnams nam mkha' lta bur ni//brjod med rgyun mi 'chad par dmigs//de don phyin ci ma log par//brjod med ting 'dzin sgom pa'i mchog/ci phyir chos kyi dbyings nyid ni//nam mkha' 
12a8(rgyud): lta bur 'dus ma byas//bsam du med pas nam mkha' 'dra//brjod du med pas gzugs dang bral//kun gzhi kun du nam mkha' la//yid ni mkha' la nyi shar 'dra//rnam shes nyon mongs sprin gyis khebs//'du byed rlung 
12b1(rgyud): gis kun bskyod de//gsal dang 'byung 'jug sangs dang 'gag/rnam shes mtshan ma'i rtog pa yis//kun gzhi nam mkha' mthong mi ster//kun gzhi mthong nyid chos sku ste//'di don nam mkha' ma brjed pa//ting 'dzin 
12b2(rgyud): chen po rgyun mi 'chad//gang zhig 'di don yid 'chad pa//gzugs kyi dngos po cir snang yang //sems ni ye nas mya ngan 'das//bde chen sangs rgyas thams cad la//dgongs pa 'di las yod mi mnga'//snang ba med 
12b3(rgyud): pa'i nam mkha' 'di//ting 'dzin chen por gnas bzhin du//'di las glags te ci zhig tshol//rgyal bas brtsal kyang yong mi brnyed//'di las gzhan du gang tshol ba//btsal ba nyid na tshi tshad gyur//gzhan nam dman 
12b4(rgyud): pa'i sgo rnams ni//ting 'dzin med pa'i chos nyid la//'dzin pa'i ting 'dzin bcug pas nor//de dag la ni gang spyod rnams//then tsher mig ni nam mkha' la//phra rab then thag 'dzud pa ltar//byis pa las gang byed pa de//
12b5(rgyud): shed can dag gis byar mi rung //shed can spyod pa'i nam mkha' de//then tsher nam mkhar shong mi 'gyur//de bas gzhan la bstan pa min//bstan na byis pa gachad gyur//chos nyid nam mkha' lta bu'i dngos//gsal bar 
12b6(rgyud): 'od stong nyi mas 'grub//byin rlabs 'od kyi zer phreng tsam//sgyu ma ltar snang mtshan mar thob//mi g.yo mtshan med rang bzhin dngos//skye med sems kyis ma mthong ba//byis pa 'drid pa'i rtsed mor snang //dngos grub chen 
12b7(rgyud): po'i sems nyid de//yid bzhin 'byung ba rin po che//ji ltar dmigs shing bsgyur bar 'gyur//grub pa'i don chen thams cad rdzogs//sems kyis sems kyi tshul mthong na//dngos grub ces de dag las 'ong //gang gis 
12b8(rgyud): stsol te su yis blang //stsal dang stsol bar byed mi dmigs//dper na chu dang me long dang //mkha' dang ral gri shel la sogs//gsal bar byed pa'i rang bzhin la//rnyog med la sogs bltas gsal ba//chu la sogs 
13a1(rgyud): @#//pa'i rang bzhin yin//de la ngo mtshar che mi 'byung //sems kyi chu gsal nam mkha' la//nyon mongs sprin gyi rnyog med na//dngos grub nyi zla skar tshogs bkram//sgrib med byin rlabs ngang gis 'char//kun gzhi 
13a2(rgyud): nam mkha'i snying po de//nyon mongs sprin gyis gting spungs pa//shes rab rlung gis ma gtong bar//ga zha mkhan gyi spyo 'dra yis//sgrib gsal chos nyid gsal 'dod pa//ji ltar chus gsher byis pa dag/nyi ma sprin ldan 
13a3(rgyud): grab ma yis//shin du sgribs par gyur pa na//sprin chen 'dus pa'i phung po la//kha yi dbugs kyi bus byas na//sprin bsal nyi 'od gsal 'dod 'dra//de phyir rnal 'byor dbang phyug gis//sems nyid don la rnam brtags te//
13a4(rgyud): kun gzhi rtsa ba shes par bya//sems nyid de la bltas gsal ba//rtsa ba dang ni gang ldan pa//dus dang tshigs dang lor gyur yang //yal ga lo 'dab 'bras bur bskrun//rtsa ba'i sa bon med mi 'gyur//ci ltar sbyor ba'i 
13a5(rgyud): rnal 'byor rnams//nan gyis mtshan pa'i dngos grub dag/byas pa'i sems kyis blang 'dod na//de yang mtshan mas de rangs 'gyur//dper na me yi rang bzhin dag/sa dang nags tshal sreg byed pa//rgyu rnams tshogs pa'i 
13a6(rgyud): bden pa las//med las yod pa'i bden par snang //de ltar mtshan ma'i rnal 'byor yang //mtshan ma'i cho 'phrul thob 'dod pa//mtshan ma'i rgyu rnams tshogs pa las//mtshan ma rangs pa'i mtshan mar snang //shin du 
13a7(rgyud): skar ma rgyal rnams la//byang chub spyod pa char spyod pa'i//dam pa'i don la cher dmigs pas//gsang mchog rig pa'i dngos grub myur//mtshan mar mi rtog rang bzhin gyis//zab mo'i don chen go ba ni//nges pa'i don 
13a8(rgyud): gyis thag gcad de//mnyam pa'i don la yid ches bya//ji ltar mkha' li tshag tsam du//ngang du brjod pas rjod byed kyi//brjod pa'i tha snyad dang bral bas//ji ltar 'khrigs kyi smig rgyu dag/snang ba nyid na med pa ltar//
13b1(rgyud): chos so cog gi rang bzhin gsal//gsal ba nyid na de mi dmigs//thabs chen gting brab shes rab lam//spros bral mtshan ma'i 'phrang las 'das//gtos ches yangs pas lam gnyis bral//dbus mtha' mi dmigs 
13b2(rgyud): mnyam pa'i lam//gcig tu mnyam par chud nas ni//yul med tshol ba'i sems bral de//blang dor gnyis kyi mtha' spangs nas//gnas pa med pa'i tshul gyis gnas//chos nyid dbyings su ro gcig phyir//shes dang 
13b3(rgyud): shes bya gnyis med de//ye nas shes nyid chos shes na//chos nyid gzhan nas bcal ci dgos//dper na sgyu ma'i bde sdug kyang //bsal nas gnyi ga mi dmigs ltar//'khor ba 'gyur ba'i bde sdug kyang //mnyam par 
13b4(rgyud): rang bzhin don gyis mnyam//ji ltar dus rnams thams cad dag/mnyam pa nyid du rtogs nas ni//sgros tshig rjes su ma 'brangs na//mnyam 'gyur dus rnams ga la dmigs//mkha' la ngang gis gzugs med phyir//
13b5(rgyud): dmigs su zin par mi 'gyur bzhin//sems kyis ma skyes don mi dpogs//skyon dang yon tan gnyis 'byed pa//rtogs nas gnyis kyi don spangs te//rang bzhin mnyam par don bzhag na//bsgom zhes ming du brjod 
13b6(rgyud): par zad//gnyis su lta ba ma skyes phyir//gnyis kyi 'du shes ci 'byung yang //rang byung zhi ba ma bkag chos//ye nas ma bcos don gyis gsal//ma skyes rnam dag byang chub sems//mi gnas ye shes thugs dang mnyam 
13b7(rgyud): rig dang ma rig khyad par las//'khor 'das rten dang 'brel bar snang //rnam rtog med phyir zhi ba med//'dzin par ma yin spros bral min//drang ba'i don bstan ma gtogs par//don la tshig gis brjod du med//snang yang 
13b8(rgyud): med pa'i rang bzhin la//shes blo yod pa'i sems mi 'byung //mnyam nyid stong pa'i don 'di la//don ldan stong pa'i sgro mi 'dogs//ma bcos rang bzhin zhi ba yi//mtshan ma'i rtog bral shes ldan pas//med do tsam du'ang mi 
14a1(rgyud): dmigs na//byung tshor 'dzin par ga la 'gyur//thams cad mi gnas byang chub pas//brtsal nas grub pa ma yin yang //sgyu ma sangs nas 'khrul bral bzhin//spros bral ye shes snying por gnas//'khor bar 
14a2(rgyud): rtogs pa dug yin te//'khor ba'i sdug bsngal mi shes na//dug 'thungs bde ba ga las 'ong //sbyor legs dug nyid sman du 'gyur//'khor ba'i las rnams de ltar snang //'khrul snang dug gzer bzod dka' ltar//ma rig 
14a3(rgyud): 'khor ba'i sdug bsngal snang //rmi lam sgyu 'phrul mi zad pas//shin du nyams thag gdungs ba'i tshe//'chi bdag bdud kyis zin 'dra la//mgon btsun gzhan gyis mi phan te//bdud sel rtsi dang 'dra bar ni//yang 
14a4(rgyud): dag lam mchog rtogs par bya//dug nad smyo sangs 'byang ba la//skad cig ma la mi bzod cing //sman dang sman pa'i rtsol grubs pa//sangs nas de dag mi dmigs ltar//'khor ba mi dmigs 'khrul ba la//
14a5(rgyud): rmi lam nad sel rtsol sgrub ltar//sgrub dang ting 'dzin rtsol byed kyang //'khor ba bstod rtogs nyid byang chub//rgyu ma rgyu 'bras mi slu bzhin//blun rmongs do la de ltar snang //mnyam nyid rig pa'i rtsal can dag/'khor ba 
14a6(rgyud): 'khrul pa'i tshig tsam du//ma nor lam rig blo la gsal//de la rtsol thob rlom sems med//sems nyid don dang ma mnyam na//tshig gis mnyam pas don mi dpogs//'khrul 'khor mnyam las ma g.yos kyang //
14a7(rgyud): log rtog sgyu ma'i tshul tsam snang //sems nyid ye sangs rgyas rig nas//rig pa'i don la brtson sbyor zhing //rang 'byung yon tan stsal sdud pa//'khrul par 'brel pa ga la yin//byang chub sems ldan bsan rdzogs na 
14a8(rgyud): ye shes mchog ldan mnyam nyid sku//ting 'dzin mchog ldan bla ma'i dbang //dge bsags mchog ldan lus spyod pa'i//bsod nams mchog ldan gzugs kyi sku//rnam smin tshul gyis grub mi lta//brtsal nas thob par 
14b1(rgyud): mi byed de//rgyu yi dus nyid 'bras bu'i mchog/dpyod pa'i sems nyid ting 'dzin ni//sems can dus na don la sbyor//lhun grub sa la bgrod mi byed//'khor ba'i sa shes bde ldan gnas//mi gnas 'khor ba'i sdug bsngal 
14b2(rgyud): yang //byang chub 'khor ba'i lam dang 'brel//sgyu ma'i mtshan nyid ma bkag tshul//rang byung rtog bral gcig tu mnyam//mnyam pa nyid kyi don brtsam par//chos nyid la yang chos nyid gsal//mnyam pas mnyam zhes tshig 
14b3(rgyud): brjod kyang //mnyam pa'i don dang 'thun mi 'gyur//byang chub de yang mi dmigs te//mi gnas zhes ni gang brjod cing //gnas dang dmigs pa'i gnas 'dzin pa//gzung 'dzin zhes ni de la bya//sgyu 'dur byang chub 
14b4(rgyud): sems med na//dngos bral yul med stong pa dag/mkha' la rang bzhin mi gnas zhes//bstan pa de ni yod ma yin//bsam gtan lus ni gtsor byed dang //gnas de lus kyi phyag rgya kun//mtshan mar mngon par zhen las 
14b5(rgyud): byung //mtshan med sems la bya brtsal med//smig rgyu bzhin du lus reng na//gnas dang phyag rgyar dmigs pa med//byed spyod ched du ston byed cing //bya med bya ba'i dmigs pa'ang med//de nyid ma skyes gzhi med pas//gnas 
14b6(rgyud): bder lus bcos kha na med//zhen par 'chos lam spyod byed pa//de nyid gzung 'dzin rnam rtog ste//bsam gtan lus dben dben ma yin//mkha' la sems dben dben pa'i mchog/sems nyid dben pa'i gnas 
14b7(rgyud): yin te//'di ni rnal 'byor spyod mchog gnas//bla gab ri phug gzhal med min//sems nyid dpag yangs gzhal med mchog/'di ni rmad byung nor bu'i gzhal//phyi dang nad med kun du nad//'du par 'dus pa tshogs 
14b8(rgyud): ma yin//sems la ye shes tshogs 'dus mchog/chos kun 'dus pas lus pa med//dpag med rgyal ba mnyes pa'i tshogs//kun rdzob 'byor pa yo byad min//sems nyid 'byor pa'i rdzas dang ldan//lhag pa'i rdzas 
15a1(rgyud): @#//mchog 'di shes na//bsgrub 'grub 'di la the tshom med//bsgom par byed pa sgom pa min//bsam bya dmigs su med pa ste//'di ltar sems kyi gsang sgom pa//don la sems 
15a2(rgyud): sgom ting 'dzin mchog/bsrung zhes bsrung bar bya mi dmigs//stong par sbas pa'i sems nyid de//mtshan mar 'das nas tshig shes na//bsrung ba'i gsang ba dam pa'o//tshig tu mnyam brjod mnyam mi dmigs//sems nyid 
15a3(rgyud): rang dag mnyam pa las//mnyam pa'i don nyid lhag par med//'di don mnyam shes mnyam pa'i don//brtul zhugs spyod pa spyod min te//sems la sems gzhol rol spyod pa//thabs che yangs pa spyod pa'i mchog/byang chub 
15a4(rgyud): don gyi phyi mo ru//sems kyi phyi mor ye gnas pas//gsol thob mtshan ma'i ming mi dmigs//dngos grub chen po rgyun mi 'chad//de ltar sems kyis sems mthong na//ye shes kyis ni chos nyid mthong //dus kun du ni skye 
15a5(rgyud): 'gag med//sngon ma mthong ba'i don mthong 'gyur//de yang dngos po cang mi mthong //thams cad rang bzhin med pa zhig/blo la 'gyus pa kho nar zad//de lta bu yi don mthong na//mtshan mar mthong ba ci yang med//sgyu ma tsam du 
15a6(rgyud): tshul shes nas//nam mkha' mnyam pa'i don snang 'gyur//kha cig dag ni de don la//mthong ba ci yang med 'gyur na//snang ba zhes ni ci phyir gdags//med pa'i don dang 'gal 'gyur zhes//the tshom za 'gyur de dag la//mthong 
15a7(rgyud): ba med pa'i don de nyid//yid kyi do la snang 'gyur na//snang zhes de phyir brjod par bya//de bas 'khrul pa ma zad par//nam mkha' mthong ba med pa de//de nyid yid la snang 'gyur yang //gzugs la snang bar 'gyur ba min//sems 
15a8(rgyud): nyid bden par ma mthong na//yid la mtshan med don gsal dang //mtshan ma 'khrul par rgyu ba gnyis//'dren mar rgyu byed 'bral 'gyur min//de nyid bden par mthong 'gyur na//de nas de ma thag tu yang //yid kyi rgyu ba rgyun 
15b1(rgyud): chad nas//sngar 'khrul mtshan ma'i chos bral te//des ni bden pa mthong ba yin//de bas 'dus pa chen po'i tshogs//sems ni yang dag lam nyid de//mkha' la bya lam rjes med bzhin//gol med lam zhes 'dren pas 
15b2(rgyud): bshad//de ltar bshad pa'i lam de yang //kun kyis rtogs par bya ba'i phyir//tshig tu mtshon de bshad mod kyang //dmigs med tshig gi yul bral ba//de yid rang bzhin de yin na//rang bzhin gzhi las mtshan mar g.yos//
15b3(rgyud): de la ming med ming btags rgyus//yin dang ma yin the tsom skyes//de la rtsod pa'i gzhir gyur te//de bsal don phyir mtshan ma yi//tshigs gi tshogs ki rnam grangs las//sgo mo dpag tu med par bshad//de yis des 
15b4(rgyud): don rjes bcad pa//thugs kyi glong nas bton pa yin//tshig gi sgron mas don brtsal ba//lam nyid ji ltar yin zhe na//dran byung rjes su 'brengs na ni//'khor ba'i gnas su 'khor bar 'gyur//dran 'byung g.yo ba ci 
15b5(rgyud): bkag pa//de dag 'gog pa nyid du 'gyur//dran byung med par blun pa ni//mu stegs can lam mun par 'gyur//'di zhes sems su gcig bzung na//mtshan ma rtog pa'i ri bo yin//snga phyi med ces phyang bcad na//
15b6(rgyud): me de chad pa'i mun can yin//gzhi yi don las yud tsam zhig/cir g.yos skye 'gag byung ba de//rnam grangs lam de 'phel 'gyur gyi//lam gyi chos nyid ga la 'gyur//dper na 'gro gang byis pa dag/grib ma spangs 
15b7(rgyud): nas rnam dag tshol//rang las byung ba'i rang bzhin gyis//btsal na grib med dag mi rnyed//don nyid ye nas gnas bzhin du//sems nyid sdug bsngal sna tshogs kyis//sha thang bar ni don tshol ba//don med bdun gyi las 
15b8(rgyud): yin no//don la tshig tu brjod pa ni//nam mkha' dag la rtsod pa 'dra//tshig tu sman dang dpyad bshad kyang //nad pa'i tshogs la mi phan no//sems nyid don du ma shes na//tshig tu don brjod don mi rtogs//'khor ba spangs 
16a1(rgyud): @#//nas 'das tshol ba//gzugs btang nas ni grib tshol 'dra//lam nyid ji ltar yin zhe na//ma rig ye shes tha dad min//ma rtogs rtogs pa lam du gcig/sems 'di sna tshogs par snang bas//sna tshogs 
16a2(rgyud): sems su gcig pa yin//dper na gser du gcig pa las//dbyibs dang gzugs ni tha dad par//byis pas so so'i ming du grags//grags kyang gser gyi ngo bor gcig/de bzhin sems kyi rang bzhin la//'khrul 
16a3(rgyud): dang ma 'khrul khyad par gyis//ji ltar bltas dgu der snang yang //snang yang sems las gzhan mi dmigs//sgron mar 'bar ba'i rin chen ni//gal te ngan khung sbas gyur kyang //de nyid yon tan rang snang bas//'od ni 
16a4(rgyud): mkha' la gsal bar 'gyur//de bzhin sems nyid rin chen sgron//lus ngag 'khor bar bying gyur kyang //rang byung rang zhi rab snang bas//shes rab chos nyid mkha' la gsal//sems nyid nam mkha' 'drar mtshungs te//
16a5(rgyud): kun khyab stong gsal sna tshogs 'byung //byung tshor g.yo 'gog thams cad kyang //rang bzhin gyis 'byung ngang gis zhi//mdor na ji ltar brtags bsams bzhin//'gyur bas thams cad ngo bor snang //ma gces g.yo 
16a6(rgyud): ba med nyid la//sna tshogs rgyu 'byung mi skyon zhing //cho 'phrul mi 'gag nyam nga med//ci bzhin nam mkha'i rang bzhin la//blo bzhag ma g.yengs kho nar zad//de ni skyes mchog don po che//de sems dus gsum thams 
16a7(rgyud): cad kyi//sangs rgyas 'byung ba'i ngo bo yin//de lta'i don la rang zhig nas//shes shing 'dris par gyur ba na//nges don mthong ba'i nyams drod ni//don dang mjal ba'i rtags chen yang //man ngag lung gis brgyad 'char te//
16a8(rgyud): nges par don gsum myong bar 'gyur//de dag thog tu phebs pa yin//drod chen rtags su bstan pa yin//de yi zhar las byung ba'i drod//'khor ba'i chos la snying rje yi//byang chub sems kyang skye bar 'gyur//mtshan ma 
16b1(rgyud): rtogs pa'i chos rnams la//yid mi ches pa'i 'du shes skye//chos rnams 'di ltar yin pa las//'di ltar 'khrul pa ngo mtshar che//ya mtshan dpyod pa'i 'du shes 'byung //'di don ma shes 'gro ba rnams//rtsol sgrub dka' ba'i 
16b2(rgyud): thabs chen gyis//yang dag don tshol lam re ring //bsags pa'i 'bras bu yod 'gyur yang //don de mthong bar yod mi nus//de 'dra'i 'du shes 'byung bar 'gyur//de ltar shes pa'i nyams drod ni//skal dang ldan pa'i skyes 
16b3(rgyud): bu gang //rgyud 'gros tshig gis ma krol bar//sems las chos byung 'bum phrag yas//smra ba'i sgo mor thams cad gsal//don la nges pa'i gtan tshigs dag/tshig tu mtshon te 'byin par nus//de ni chos sku mnyam nyid dang 
16b4(rgyud): dbyer med gnyis su med pa yin//brten pa'i gsung thugs sku brnyan dang //de ni mtshungs pa yod min no//zhi lus mthar 'gog skyon gnyis dang //de ni mtshungs par yod ma yin//sa la slob pa'i sems dpa' dang //de yang 
16b5(rgyud): mi tshugs rgyal bas gsungs//rig 'dzin drang srong don dpyod dang //lus 'dra sems ni yod mi tshungs//de phyir rnam dag lam chen ni//sems kyi gsang 'di yin pas na//skal ldan 'dus pa chen po 'tshogs//lam 'di 
16b6(rgyud): shes par bya ba las//btsal bar bya ba'i rgyu med de//lam 'di shes pa de nyid kyis//dka' ba'i brtson sgrub ma byas par//thams cad thams cad grub par 'gyur//'dus pa chen po'i tshogs de bas ni//sems 'di ni lam 
16b7(rgyud): zhes bya'o//'khor ba zhes bya'o//sems can zhes bya'o//gru zhes bya'o//bgrod pa zhes bya'o//byang chub ces bya'o//sems zhes bya'o//shes rab ces bya'o//ting nge 'dzin ces bya'o//gnas shes bya'o//rab ces bya'o//mchog ces bya'o//
16b8(rgyud): phul zhes bya'o//bla na med pa zhes bya'o//zab pa zhes bya'o//gsang ba zhes bya'o//gting dpag dka' ba zhes bya'o//chos zhes bya'o//byang chub sems dpa' zhes bya'o//sangs rgyas shes bya'o//de ci'i phyir zhe na/'jug pa'i phyir ni 
17a1(rgyud): @#//lam mo//'khrul pa'i phyir ni 'khor ba'o//ma shes pa'i phyir ni sems can no//sgrol ba'i phyir ni gru'o//bskyod pa'i phyir ni dgrod pa'o//bde ba'i phyir ni byang chub bo//dran pa nges pa'i phyir ni sems so//mkhas 
17a2(rgyud): pa'i phyir ni shes rab bo//gsal ba'i phyir ni ting nge 'dzin to//phyin pa'i phyir ni gnas so//zla med pa'i phyir ni rab bo//ngo mtshar ba'i phyir ni mchog go//'phags pa'i phyir ni phul lo//dbang byed pa med pa'i phyir ni bla na med pa'o//
17a3(rgyud): gting ring ba'i phyir ni zab pa'o//gab pa'i phyir ni gsang ba'o//phra ba'i phyir ni gting dpag dka' ba'o//kun 'byung ba'i phyir ni chos so//sa non pa'i phyir ni byang chub sems dpa'o//mthar phyin pa'i phyir ni sangs rgyas te//sems 
17a4(rgyud): dpa' chen po de'i phyir na rnal 'byor gyi lam 'di shes par bya ste/lam 'di ni chos thams cad kyi gzhi'o//sangs rgyas thams cad kyi yum mo//de ci'i phyir zhe na/chos thams cad 'di las 'byung ba'i phyir na bzhi'o//mthar 
17a5(rgyud): phyin pa'i sangs rgyas kyi lam 'di nas gshegs par gyur to//de ltar stong pa nyid kyang lam 'di nas gshegs pa ste/de'i phyir lam 'di shes par bya'o//de ltar na de'i phyir lam 'di ni de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad 
17a6(rgyud): kyi yab ces kyang bya'o//de bzhin du yum zhes kyang bya ba dang /sras zhes bya ba dang bdag po zhes bya ba dang /gdung zhes bya ba dang srog ces bya ba dang /nor zhes bya ba dang /bran zhes bya ba dang /sku zhes bya ba dang /gsung zhes 
17a7(rgyud): bya ba dang thugs zhes bya ba dang /yon tan zhes bya ba dang /phrin las chen po zhes bya ba'o//de ci'i phyir zhe na/thabs chen po dang shes rab chen po dang /byang chub chen po dang /dbang chen po dang /de bzhin nyid dang /'da' bar dka' ba dang /chos nyid 
17a8(rgyud): la zad mi shes pa dang /snying rje chen po dang /rnam par brjid pa dang /sna tshogs su ston pa dang /thams cad du mkhyen pa dang /cir yang 'byung ba dang /mdzad pa sna tshogs pa'i phyir de dag ni de ltar yin par shes par bya'o//de'i phyir yang 
17b1(rgyud): lam 'di yongs su shes par bya'o//de bzhin du 'dod pa chen po zhes kyang bya'o//'tsho ba chen po zhes bya ba dang /longs spyod chen po zhes bya ba dang yo byad chen po zhes bya ba dang/rgyu zhes bya ba dang /rkyen zhes bya ba dang /'bras bu zhes 
17b2(rgyud): bya ba dang spyod pa zhes bya ba dang /cho 'phrul zhes bya ba dang /phyag rgya zhes bya ba dang /bsnyen pa zhes bya ba dang /nye ba'i bsnyen pa zhes bya ba dang/bsgrub pa zhes bya ba dang /bsgrub pa chen po zhes bya ba dang tshogs chen po zhes bya ba dang
17b3(rgyud): kun du rdzogs pa zhes bya'o//de ci'i phyir zhe na/yul la 'dod pa sna tshogs pa dang /dmigs pas 'tsho ba dang /'jol nyog tu spyod pa dang /dgos pa sgrub pa dang /'bras bur byed pa dang /kun du log pa dang /don grub pa dang /
17b4(rgyud): smin par bsags pa dang /rang bzhin du gyur pa dang /bya ba byed pa dang /sna tshogs su 'byung ba dang /mtshan mar bstan pa dang /rnam par 'dud pa dang /shin du mdzad pa dang /'dod pa byed pa dang /lhag par 'dod pa dang /kun du 'byor 
17b5(rgyud): ba dang /lhun gyis grub pa'i phyir yongs su shes par bya'o//gzhan yang 'dus pa chen po'i tshogs 'di la ni thams cad yod de/chos thams cad ni rnal 'byor rnam pa gsum du 'dus par shes par bya'o//gsum gang zhe na/
17b6(rgyud): rgyu'i rnal 'byor dang /rkyen kyi rnal 'byor dang /'bras bu'i rnal 'byor rnam pa gsum yang 'di nyid yin/zhing 'di las 'byung bar shes par bya te/de yang ji ltar zhe na/rgyu ni 'khrul pa gcig shes te chub par 'dod pas

24a4(rgyud): pa'i mtha'//gom par bya ba rab tu gces//ji ltar glang po chur 'jug pa//tsham tshom chags thogs yod med par//dga' ba'i longs spyod brod tshul gyis//mi ldog pha rol phyin pa ltar//rnal 'byor nges par spyod 
24a5(rgyud): pa'i blo//brtul zhugs rgya mtsho'i chur 'jug pa//rtsal chen spyod pa'i brod tshul gyis//chags thogs med par dor ba'o//bslab par bya ba'i mtha' yin no//spyod pa'i bslab pa dam pa ni//goms par bya ba rab tu 
24a6(rgyud): gces//ji ltar khyung chen mkha' lding la//bshog pa'i rtsal gyis nam 'phang spyod//dam grog chu klung mtha' yas kyang //gshog rlabs 'og tu kun chud ltar//rnal 'byor mnyam nyid mkha' lding ba//theg pa'i spyod sgo 
24a7(rgyud): mtha' yas kyang //mnyam pa chen po don gyis ni//lta 'phang rlabs kyis thag gcad pa'o//'di ni re dang dogs pa yi//the tsom gcod pa'i lta ba ste//don la sems kyis blta ba ste//bslab pa 'di dag goms pa gces//
24a8(rgyud): dper na rgya mtshor nor len mi//sprul dang chu srin sbrang ma las//sogs pa gdug pa'i sdug bsngal kun//dka' ba nyams su blangs nas grub//de bzhin rnal 'byor bde tshol ba//rnam rtog bying rmugs phyar g.yeng dgra 
24b1(rgyud): gzhom par dka' ba'i las 'di dag/nyams su blangs na bde thob rnyed//'di ni ting 'dzin dam pa yi//nyams su blangs pa'i bslab pa ste//sems kyi goms par bya ba'i las//'dris par bya ba rab tu gces//ji ltar 
24b2(rgyud): mun pa dug dang rdzas//sman snang gser du bsgyur bas na//'gyur rtsi man ngag med pa'i mis//dka' ba'i las chen sna tshogs pas//sdug bsngal spyad kyang mi 'grub ste//su zhig man ngag ldan pa'i mis//tshegs med 
24b3(rgyud): gyi na'i tshul gyis na//bsgyur bas sman snang gser 'gyur ltar//rnal 'byor chen po'i don sgrub pa//kun gzhi gnas 'gyur man ngag dang //gang dag mi ldan pas kyang ni//dka' ba spyad kyang 'grub pa min//sems kyi 
24b4(rgyud): gsang 'di sus mthong ba//de dag man ngag ldan pa ste//'dod pa'i don mchog gang yin pa//de dag tshegs med gyi nas 'grub//don rnams sgrub par gang byed pa//man ngag can gyis 'grub pa'i phyir//don rnams 
24b5(rgyud): 'grub par 'gyur ba yi//bslab pa 'di dag rab tu gces//dper na kun du rgyu ba'i mi//'dod pa'i yul du 'gro byed na//yul gyi rnam pa mtha' yas kyang //bgrod pa'i lam gnas gcig las med//de bzhin bsam 
24b6(rgyud): gyis mi khyab pa'i//brgyad khri bzhi stong chos bstan kyang //bkri zhing drang ba'i tshig yin te//bsam pa'i yul ni gcig pa'o//don rnams gcig tu sdud pa yi//bslab par bya ba dam pa ste//nges par gcig las 
24b7(rgyud): med pa'i phyir//'di dag goms par bya ba yin//bslab pa 'di la goms gyur na//lobs pa'i yon tan mtha' yas te//lobs pas lam la mkhas par 'gyur//sngags kyi yon tan chen po yang //rmad du byung 
24b8(rgyud): ba thob par 'gyur//gsang sngags chen po'i khyad par ni//thams cad thams cad bya'o cog/byin gyi rlabs kyi spyod pa 'am//dbang sgyur thabs kyi spyod pa ste//dper na nam mkha' rgya mtsho 'am//ji ltar me dpung 
25a1(rgyud): @#//rgyal po 'dra//de yang de yul che chung gis//khyad par nyid kyis brlabs pa yin//rgyal po de yang khyad par gyis//dbang sgyur bya ba'i khams rnams la//che chung rim pa yod pa yin//rnal 'byor sngags

25a4(rgyud): ye shes dang //sgrib dang gol sa rtog pa dang //spyod dang dam tshig ting 'dzin dang //dus dang dngos grub mthar phyin dang //lta ba'i khyad par gnas gyur rnams//thams cad gcig las med na yang //grangs dang dbye ba'i bye 
25a5(rgyud): brag rnams//snod kyi rtsal dang sbyar nas ni//chos rnams kun gzhir gcig pa la//kun gzhi gnas 'gyur rnam pa lngas//bye brag lam gyi chos su snang //gzhi nyid rgyu yi kun gzhi dang //sbyor ba 'dun pa'i kun gzhi 
25a6(rgyud): dang //snang ba don gyi kun gzhi dang //'gyur ba med pa'i kun gzhi dang //brtsal med gnas pa'i kun gzhi lnga//skabs lnga dag tu gnas 'gyur bas//sa yi khyad par bgrod pa yi//de yang gnyis kyi chos 'di dag/kun kyang

25a8(rgyud): chung ngu rnams//rang rang rtsa ba bor nas ni//rtsal chen rdzogs pa'i rnal 'byor las//sogs ba'i rnal 'byor chen po'i tshul//spyod par byad dang don 'dod pa//sbal bus mkha' lding las spyad kyang //rtsal ba'i yul ni 
25b1(rgyud): chad pa yin//nor bu las kyi 'phang dmas pa//rang skal 'tsho ba'i ched bor nas//rgyal po thabs kyis 'phang mtho ba'i//longs spyod che la dmigs bcas kyang //de dag mi 'thob 'tsho bral bzhin//skal ldan can gyi 
25b2(rgyud): don 'di dag/skal zhan dag gis 'grub par dka'o//sna tshogs gter gyi khang pa dag/rgyal po'i mdzod du gyur pas na//spyod sgo 'jug pa thams cad kyang //dka' thub phyi nang khyad par gyis//rin chen sna dgu'i rim 
25b3(rgyud): pa dag/so so'i las thabs mthon dman gyis//yul de gcig la med na yang //yig tshad sgo dgu so sor spyad//rgyal por gyur pa de kun la//dbang sgyur byed pa'i bdag po yin//mi gnas chos kyi dbyings nyid 
25b4(rgyud): dag/blta ba'i yul du gyur pa na//rig pa rtsal gyi khyad par gyis//blta yul gcig las med na yang //so so 'jug sgo kun nas spyod//dbyings kyi rang bzhin gcig pa la//rtsal 'phang mthon dman khyad par gyis//
25b5(rgyud): skas kyi gdang bu rtsib ltar//sa dang theg pa'i khyad par du//mthon dman rim par snang ba yin//mnyam par gyur pa'i gzhi gcig la//'thun dang mi mthun don gyis ni//dper na khang pa sgo mangs bzhin//sa dang theg 
25b6(rgyud): pa so so'i sgo//mi mthun so sor snang ba yin//so so'i theg pa'i sgo rnams la//'jug pa la sogs ma sbyangs kyang //kun gyi rtsa ba 'dir bcad pas//gcig te tha dad yongs kyis med//de ni ri dang lhun po 'am//kun 
25b7(rgyud): du rgyu dang gzhon nu ltar//mthong thos ston pa'i khyad par ni//don gyis de ltar yod pa yin//des na chos rnams thams cad kyi//rtsa ba sems 'di yin pas na//nam mkha' lta bu'i dpes mtshon te//kun gyi rtsa ba shes
