30a3(rgyud): as ni chos nyid mtshon par byed//a las yig 'bru'i tshogs 'phros te//tshig tshogs bstan pa rgya mtshor grags//yi ge'i sgra skad kun bzung ste//sna tshogs yi ge'i skad du bsgyur//yig 'bru'i yan lag mang po rnams//
30a4(rgyud): rang bzhin a yi ngang du 'dus//thugs kyi klong nas a byung bas//a yi 'od zer 'phro 'du las//mgur gyi gsang bar shar ba las//gsung sgra yi ge lngar 'phros pa//a ka kha ga g+ha nga /de nas a yi 'od zer de//ljags 
30a5(rgyud): kyi zla gam pad mor 'phros//ljags kyi mthil du 'khyil ba las//'od zer yig 'bru lngar shar ba//tsa tsha dza dz+ha nya/de nas a yi 'od zer te//ljags kyi rtse la 'phros pa las//'od zer yig 'bri bcur shar ba//Ta Tha Da D+ha Na ta 
30a6(rgyud): tha da d+ha na//de nas a yi 'od zer de nas//zhal gyi sgo nas 'phros pa las//cho 'phrul yi ge lngar shar ba//ba pha ba b+ha ma/de nas a yi 'od zer da//phyi rol yul du 'phros pa las//cho 'phru yi ge sna tshogs 'phros//ya war 
30a7(rgyud): la sha Sha sa ha k+Sha//i I u U e e o o a aH//a las cho 'phrul 'od 'phros pas//yi ge'i phreng ba tshogs byung ste//yi ge yan lag ye shes la//sangs rgyas sku yi sa bon byung //dpag tu med pa'i rgyal ba rnams//
30a8(rgyud): sku dang gsung thugs sprul pa'i tshogs//phyag rgya sna tshogs mdzes par snang //des na yi ge'i rang bzhin ni//rgyal ba'i sku nyid yin par bshad//a ni thugs kyi sa bon te//ka la sogs pa dang po lnga//mgur gyi gsang ba'i 
30b1(rgyud): rang bzhin yin//gnyis pa rtsa la sogs pa lnga//zla gam ljags kyi rang bzhin yin//gsum pa Ta la sogs pa bcu//ljags kyi rtse mo'i rang bzhin yin//bzhi pa pa la sogs pa lnga//zhal gyi sgros las byung bar bshad//
30b2(rgyud): lnga pa ya la sogs pa yi//bcu tshan gnyis kyi yi ge dag/sku las 'phros par rab tu bshad//zhabs kyi 'khor lo'i 'od zer las//sgrib pa sreg pa'i yig 'bru tu//gsang ba'i mkha' yi 'od zer las//cho 'phrul 'byung 
30b3(rgyud): ba'i yig 'bru k+Sha//sku yi khru na tshad lte ba las//'dod pa 'byung ba'i yig 'bru hwa//thugs kar 'khyil ba'i dpal be'u las//mtshan ma dag pa'i yig 'bru sha//phyag gi 'khor lo'i 'od zer las//cho 'phrul ston pa'i yig 
30b4(rgyud): 'bru shu//skyes bu chen po'i dpung pa las//rtog pa 'joms pa'i yig 'bru la//skyes bu chen po'i lta zur las//'dod chags sreg pa'i yi ge ra//skyes bu chen po'i sku skabs las//gti mug sel ba'i yi ge wa//skyes bu 
30b5(rgyud): chen po'i byin pa las//'khor ba gnon pa'i yig 'bru ya//skyes bu chen po'i spyan gnyis las//rang bzhin yi ge i gnyis shar//skyes bu chen po'i snyan gnyis las//rang bzhin/yi ge u gnyis shar//skyes bu chen
30b6(rgyud): po'i shangs gnyis las//rang bzhin ye ge e gnyis shar//skyes bu chenapo'i tshems gnyis las//rang bzhi na yi ge o gnyis shar//skyes bu che na po'i spyi gtsug nas//rgyal po'i sa bon oM du shar//des na yig
30b7(rgyud): 'bru'i yan lag kun//rgyal ba rgya mtsho'i sku gsung thugs//oM ni sku yi rang bzhin te//AHni thugs kyi mtshan nyid do//yi ge'i phreng ba mang po kun//gsung brjod gsung gi rang bzhin no//a ni chos sku'i rang bzhin te/
30b8(rgyud): oM ni longs spyod rdzogs pa'i sku//yig 'bru 'bar ba'i phreng ba rnams//sprul pa 'dren pa'i sku zhes bshad//a ni chos kyi dbyings yin te//oM ni rig pa'i ye shes yin//yig 'bru 'bar ba'i preng ba rnams//chos
31a1(rgyud): @#//
31b1(rgyud): byed pa'i sgo//mi dmigs thams cad grol bar ston//ga ni don du gyur pa sgo//yul med thams cad grol bar ston //g+ha ni gnas pa'i sgo yin te//gcig tu ma nges grol bar ston//nga ni 'dzin pa med pa'i sgo//
31b2(rgyud): 'dzin bral thams cad grol bar ston//tsa ni 'gag pa med pa'i sgo//cir yang snang bas thams cad dgrol//tsha ni 'da' ba med pa'i sgo//'bral ba med pas thams cad 'grol//dza ni ma byung ba yi sgo//mi gnas pas ni thams
31b3(rgyud): cad grol//dz+ha ni rnyog ma bral ba'i sgo//dri ma dag pas thams cad grol//nya ni spyod dang bral ba'i sgo//bya ba med pas thams cad grol//Ta ni nga rgyal med pa'i sgo//ri bo med pas thams cad grol //tha ni sgyu rtsal med pa'i sgo/
31b4(rgyud): byed pa med pas thams cad grol//Da ni 'dres pa med pa'i sgo//rtsod pa med pas thams cad sgrol//D+ha ni slu ba med pa'i sgo//sgyu ma thams cad grol bar byed//Na ni yul dang bral ba'i sgo//'dzin pa med pas thams cad
31b5(rgyud): grol//ta ni tha rnyad med pa'i sgo//brjod pa med pas thams cad dgrol//tha ni rten dang bral ba'i sgo//gnas pa med pa thams cad grol//da ni gtong dang bral ba'i sgo//len pa med pas thams cad 
31b6(rgyud): grol//mtshan nyid med pas thams cad grol/ /na ni yul dang bral ba'i so//'dzin pa med pas tham sa cad grol//pa ni nges par 'byung ba'i sgo//'gag pa med pas thams cad  grol//pha ni dngos po med pa'i sgo//ci yang
31b7(rgyud): med pas thams cad grol//ba ni brjod pa med pa'i sgo//brjod pa mad pas thams cad grol//b+ha ni rdul dang bral ba'i sgo//rtog pa med pas thams cad grol //ma ni chags pa med pa'i sgo//rtag pa med pas thams cad
31b8(rgyud):  grol//ya ni chags pa med pa'i so//gos pa med pas thams cad rol//wa ni rgyu rkyen med pa'i sgo//'bras bu med pas thams cad grol//ra ni rkyen dang bral ba'i sgo//dor ba med pas thams cad grol //la ni blang ba med
32a1(rgyud): @#//pa'i sgo//yon tan med pas thams cad grol//sha ni spros pa med pa'i sgo//zhi bas thams cad grol bar byed//Sha ni khur dang bral ba'i sgo//lci ba med pas thams cad grol////sa ni yang dag gsang ba'i sgo//
32a2(rgyud): nges pas thams cad grol bar byed //ha ni rgyu dang bral ba'i sgo//rkyen dang bral bas grol bar byed//a ni khams dang bral ba'i sgo//mtshan nyid med pas grol bar byed//i ni mthong ba med pa'i sgo//gzugs dang bral 
32a3(rgyud): bas thams cad grol//u ni thos pa med pa'i sgo//sgra dang bral bas thams cad grol//e ni tshor ba med pa'i sgo//dri dang bral bas grol bar byad//o ni tshor ba med pa'i sgo////reg dang bral bas thams cad 
32a4(rgyud): grol//o ni cho 'phrul 'byung ba'i sgo//cir yang snang bas thams cad grol/mya ngan 'das dang 'khor ba'i chos//yod mthong snang grags chos thams cad//yi ge'i tshogs su mngon sangs rgyas//yi ger ma gyur gang yang med//
32a5(rgyud): chos nyid stong pa'i nyi ma la//'od zer yig 'bru'i tshogs su 'bar//chos nyid nam mkha' lta bu la//yig 'bru sna tshogs phra bkod pa//dus gsum rgyal ba'i dkyil 'khor rdzogs//nyi ma 'bar ba'i 'od zer las//'od 
32a6(rgyud): kyi 'bar ba'i rdo rje 'bar//yi ge hU~M gi 'od 'phros pas//rdo rje snying po'i sa bon zhes//rdo rje rigs kyi zhing du grags//nyi ma'i 'od zer rin chen las//yig 'bru 'od kyi swa 'phros pas//rin chen snying po'i sa bon zhes//
32a7(rgyud): rin chen rigs kyi zhing du grags//nyi ma pad mo 'bar 'phros pas//cho 'phrul yi ge'i oM du 'bar//pad ma rigs kyi zhing du grags/sna tshogs nyi ma'i 'od 'phros pas//cho 'phrul ye ge har gsal bas//sna tshogs 
32a8(rgyud): snying po'i sa bon te//las kyi rigs kyi zhing du grags/dus gsum rgyal ba thams cad kyi//thugs ni rdo rje ye shes yin//ye shes dam pas sprul pa yi//rgyal ba dpag tu med pa la//rdo rje rigs shes bya bar bshad//
32b1(rgyud): yon tan ye shes rin chen yin//rin chen de las sprul pa yi//rgyal ba dpag tu med pa la//rin chen rigs shes bya bar bshad//gsung gi ye shes pad mo ste//pad mo ye shes las sprul ba'i//rgyal ba dpag tu med
32b2(rgyud): pa la//pad mo rigs kyi zhing du grags//phrin las bya ba sna tshogs te//sna tshogs ye shes sprul pa yi//rgyal ba dbag tu med pa la//las kyi rigs shes bya bar bshad //sku ni ma bcos de bzhin nyid//de bzhin nyid kyi
32b3(rgyud): ye shes la//sprul pa rgyal ba dpag med la//de bzhin rigs shes bya bar bshadag/de bas rigs kyi gtan tshigs ni//ma bcos sku ni de bzhin nyid//mi 'gyur rigs ni rdo rje'i rigs//yon tan kun 'byung rin chen
32b4(rgyud): rigs//chags med gsung gi pad mo'i rigs//bya ba byed pa las kyi rigs//sku yi sprul pa thams cad ni//de bzhin        rigs kyi gdung rgyud 'dzin//thugs las sprul pa'i sangs rgyas ni//rdo rje rigs kyi gdung 
32b5(rgyud): rgyud 'dzin//yon tan yul gyi sangs rgyas rnams//rin chen rigs kyi gdung rgyud 'dzin //gsung las sprul pa'i sangs rgyas rnams//pad ma rigs kyi gdung rgyud 'dzini//mdzad pa sprul pa'i sangs rgyas rnam+sa/
32b6(rgyud): phrin las rigs kyi gdung rgyud 'dzin//ci phyir rigs shes bya zhe na/dang po de las byung ba dang//bar du de yi bya ba byed//tha mar de yi rgyud 'dzin phyir//de phyir rigs shes grags pa yin//dper na me dang chu dag dang//nam mkha' 
32b7(rgyud): dang ni sa dag la//gnas pa'i sems can mtha' dag kun//gnas rgyun nyid kyi ming 'dzin de//me la gnas pa'i sems can la//me yi yi dgas ma zhes zer//chu la gnas pa'i sems can  la//chu srin twa ra na zhes zer//
32b8(rgyud): ci phyir lnga zhes bya zhe na//rgyal ba sangs rgyas thams cad la//sku dang gsung thugs yon tan dang//mdzad pa phrin las nyid dang lnga//rig pa lnga dang lnga nyid du//thams cad kun tu gsal bar snang//des na lnga zhes nges par 
33a1(rgyud): @#//bshad//ci phyir zhe na sangs rgyas kun//lnga po de las ma gtogs pa//gzhan las byung zhing gzhan na med//sangs rgyas mtshon gyi khyad par yang //mtshan med pa las mtshan btags pa//thams cad lnga po nyid 
33a2(rgyud): la btags//mtshan gyi bye brag thams cad kun//tha snyad tsam du gdags par zad//rigs med pa las rigs smos pa//'dul ba'i dbang du lngar snang yang //dran pa'i don te dngos gzhi med//ci yang med las ci 
33a3(rgyud): snang ba//mkha' la 'ja' tshon byung ba 'dra//chos kun nam mkha' 'dra ba zhes//gsang ba mchog gi bdag pos bshad//mnyam dang mi mnyam mnyam pa yin//sangs rgyas med cing sems can med//'khor ba med 
33a4(rgyud): cing chos nyid med//dmigs pa med de mi dmigs med//de ltar shes pa mkhas pa yin//rig dang ma rig tshul gcig ste//de ltar shes pa rgyal ba yin//sems can rang bzhin sangs rgyas nyid//sangs rgyas 
33a5(rgyud): rang bzhin sems can te//sems can sangs rgyas gcig pa yin//de ltar shes pa rgyal ba'i sras//byang chub phyir ni gang ma bstes//ma brnyes gang yang de ma brnyes//brnyes pa med pa'i don mthong na//de ni rnyes pa'i 
33a6(rgyud): mchog yin no//gzhan yang 'dus pa'i rnal 'byor rnams//lam gyi ngo bo tshol 'dod na//lam gyi rtags nyid dpes mtshon te//lam gyi don la goms par bya//mi gnas mig ldan sgron ma yis//lam gyi rgyu la 
33a7(rgyud): mkhas par bya//bag chags rtog pa'i mtshan ma rnams//sna tshogs dngos por snang mod kyang //nam mkha' dag la smig rgyu 'dra//de yang gtan tshigs nges pa yi//de yi mtshon te rtogs bya na//mkha' la sprin phung 
33a8(rgyud): 'ja' mtshon dam chang la sogs pa'i rgyun snang yang //nam mkha'i cho 'phrul gcig tu zad//rtag tu de ltar snang ba min//dper na sgyu ma'i rang bzhin yang //slu zhing 'drid pa cir snang yang //sgyu ma'i 'phrul pa snang bar 
33b1(rgyud): zad//rgyag tu da ltar snang ba min//dper na rdo yi dngos po yang //sra zhing 'khregs pa'i dngos po yang //rtags sam phye mar byas nas kyang //rlung gi mthu yis gtor byas na//de la rang bzhin ga la yod//dper na me yi 
33b2(rgyud): rang bzhin dag/tshang tshing spar spur kun sreg cing //'bar 'phro snang gsal mtshang byed na//chu ye mthu yis rlag 'gyur na//de la rang bzhin gal yod//dper na rdzas kyi dngos po yang //me yi mthu yis sreg 'gyur na//
33b3(rgyud): de la rang bzhin ga la yong //shes pas zin pa'i sems can rnams//gzugs dang dran pa sna tshogs kyi//dbang po la sogs mdzes kyur kyang //phung po mi rtag shi 'phos na//dngos po brten par mi 'gyur te//gzugs 
33b4(rgyud): rnams so sor zhig nas ni//rus pa dag kyang mi snang ste//zhig dang rul la sogs pa yis//tha mar ci yang mi snang 'gyur//ri bo chen po la sogs pa//dngos po brtan g.yo'i 'jig rten rnams//sreg pa'i bskal pa 
33b5(rgyud): la sogs pa//bskal pa gsum gyis brlag byas na//de la rang bzhin ga la yod//des na dngos po'i chos rnams kun//dngos por brtags pa ma yin pas//chos rnams rang bzhin med par shes//'di ni rnal 'byor lam 
33b6(rgyud): gyi mchog/'dus pa'i tshogs rnams goms par gyis//zhes sems dpa'i rdo rje smra ba'i seng ges rnal 'byor mchog gi lam gsungs pa'o////rgyud kyi rgyal po chen po rdo rje bkod pa kun 'dus rig pa'i mdo las rnal 
33b7(rgyud): 'byor rjes su 'jug pa'i le'u ste gnyis pa'o////de nas smra ba'i seng ge sems dpa'i rdo rje de tsho 'phrul chen po sna tshogs ston pa zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs so//ting nge 'dzin de'i byin gyi rlabs 
33b8(rgyud): las ni rab thams cad g.yos par gyur te/don yongs su rdzogs pa'i lnga ltas byung bar gyur to//'khor der 'dus par gyur pa rnams la la ni nyi zla'i steng na gnas shing chos nyid kyi nam mkha' la lding bar gyur to//
34a1(rgyud): @#//la la ni ri rab rgyang mar 'phen//la la ni stong gsum chen po'i khams 'phen par gyur to//la la ni chos nyid bden pa'i don mthong bar gyur to//la la ni seng ge'i khri la 'dug par gyur to//de nas 
34a2(rgyud): sems dpa'i rdo rje de cho 'phrul gyi ting nge 'dzin de las bzhengs te 'dus pa chen po'i rnal 'byor gyis shin tu ngo mtshar che bar gyur nas sems dpa'i rdo rje de la rnal 'byor gyi phyag lus kun tu sa la brdabs nas 'di 
34a3(rgyud): skad ces gsol to//sems dpa' chen po cho 'phrul rgya cher gda' ba 'di don ji lta bu lags//rtags ji lta bu lags/bdag cag rnams la rgya cher bshad du gsol//zhes zhus pa las smra ba'i seng ge chen pos bka' 
34a4(rgyud): stsal pa/'dus pa'i rnal 'byor rnams 'di dag ni don yongs su rdzogs pa'i ltas chen po yin te/snga ltas kyi mtshan ma 'di dang sbyar na 'dus pa chen po rnams sa nas sar shin tu 'phar zhing yang dag nges pa'i don mtho 
34a5(rgyud): ba'i snga ltas yin no zhes gsungs pa las/rnal 'byor gyi tshogs kyis gsol pa/sems dpa' chen pos non pa'i snga ltas ni ji lta bu lags/don mthong ba'i snga ltas ni ji lta bu la gas/bdag cag rnams la bshad 
34a6(rgyud): du gsol zhes zhus pa las/sems dpa' rdo rjes bka' stsal pa/'dus pa chen po'i tshogs legs so//khyod kyis zhus pa'i don rgya cher bshad par bya'o//rna ba lhogs la gus par nyon cig/sgrub pa don gyi rnal 'byor pa 
34a7(rgyud): rnal 'byor don la sbyor ba'i tshe/spyad pa'i las kyi khyad par gyis//khams dang sa ni gar 'gro ba//mdo don tshul lngas lung bstan no//nges pa'i lnga ltas byung ba yi//'bras bu don gyi khyad par yang //cho 'phrul sna tshogs ston 
34a8(rgyud): par nus//de nas yang dag bden pa 'thob nges pa'i snga ltas rnam pa lnga//rgyu yi dus na 'byung ba ni//tshig gi brjod pa 'di lta ste//blo rtsal las kyi khyad par yang //snang ba lus kyi snga ltas dang //ngo mtshar rdzas rnyed tshul 
34b1(rgyud): rtags dang //mtshan ma pra yi rmi ltas dang //'dun pa mang po'i snga ltas kyis//'bras bu gang dang gang 'grub pa//mtshan ma gsal bar rgyud las 'byung //dper na me yi snga ltas ni//ngu ba'i mtshan ma rgyas par 'byung //
34b2(rgyud): ngu ba gar byung gnas par de na//me nyid yod par nges pa yin//de bzhin chu yi lnga ltas kyang //rlangs dang sngags dang srog chags 'byung //de na chu nyid yod par nges//rlung gi ltas kyang 'di lta ste//sprin rnams rgyug cing ngu 
34b3(rgyud): bur 'chang //des na rlung chen 'byung bar nges//rtsi 'tshal lo ma gang byung ba//de na gser khung yod pa ltar//snga ltas mtshan ma 'di dag gis//'bras bu mthon dman gar 'gyur ba//rgyu la mkhas par byas nas ni//mtshan 
34b4(rgyud): ma ci byung de la sbyar//bar chad med pa'i brtson 'grus gyis//srog la bab kyang mi blta par//rnal 'byor yul la 'jug pa dang //ting 'dzin rig pa'i rtsal sprugs la//gnyen po kun bsten sa sbyangs nas//rnal 
34b5(rgyud): 'byor sa la brtson par bya//'dis ni gdon mi za ba dang //la la the tshom mi skye zhing //ma nges la zhugs the tsom med//dper na 'jig rten byis pa dag/yung rings ma la skyo ba ltar//snga ltas byung ba'i sgra thos na//shin tu bros 
34b6(rgyud): pas mdun bsu ltar//ltas chen byung ba'i brod tshul gyis//shin tu brtson pas bde thob 'gyur//de bas snga ltas mtshan ma rnams//shes nas brtson par 'bad pa'i rigs//blo rtsal las kyi khyad par gyis//ting 'dzin 
34b7(rgyud): drod kyi snga ltas 'byung //yang dag bden pa'i don bstan na//mi skrag mi dngang dga' ba skye//yang dag don la nges bltas na//'khor ba'i chos la skyo ba skye//yang dag don las 'dris pa dang //rnam grangs chos la dad mi 
34b8(rgyud): 'gyur//yang dag don nyid mthong ba na//tshig la dga' ba mi skye zhing //don nyid nye bar 'dzin par byed//don mthong ba dang ye shes skye//ye shes skyes par gyur pa dang //dngos por snang ba thams cad kun//sgyu ma bzhin du 
35a1(rgyud): @#//shes par 'gyur//sgyu ma tsam du shes pa la//brten nas chos nyid don bltas na//snang ba med pa'i don nyid ni//snang ba med par mthong bar 'gyur//snang med srid pa mthong ba'i tshe//cho 'phrul rgyu ba thams cad 'byung //
35a2(rgyud): de nyid bden pa'i snga ltas so//de nas nyi ma'i 'od zer gyi//rdul phran 'dra ba'i mtshan ma 'byung //de ni g.yos pa'i snga ltas so//de nas/smig rgyu lta bur 'byung //dang po'i drod ces bya bar bshad//de nas 'tsho yi rlabs ltar 'byung //
35a3(rgyud): bar gyi drod ces bya bar bshad //de nas rlung chen lta bur yang //thogs pa med par 'byung bar 'gyur//tha ma'i drod ces bya bar bshad/de nas yig 'bru'i drod spros pas//char chen 'bab pa lta bur 'byung //de ni thob pa'i drod 
35a4(rgyud): ces bya//de nas mkha' la sprin ldings bzhin//ting 'dzin rtsal 'phangs mtho bar 'gyur//de ni shin du brten pa'i drod//de nas ta la gcig tsam du//bdag gi lus ni dbang med par//mkha' la 'phar zhing 'phags par 
35a5(rgyud): rtsol//'di ni shin du gsal ba'i drod//de nas sa chen g.yo ba ltar//shin du yang yom mar mer 'byung //'di ni dang po'i rtags shes bya//de nas ting 'dzin rtsal sbyangs na//ci ltar dmigs pa der 'grub ste//me yi skye 
35a6(rgyud): mched der bsgoms na//me yi rang bzhin nyid du 'gyur//chu yi skye mched der bsgoms na//chu yi skye mched nyid du 'gyur//rlung gi skye mched der bsgoms na//rlung gi skye mched nyid du 'gyur//yig 'bru'i skye mched der 
35a7(rgyud): bsgoms na//yig 'bru gang ltar gsal bar 'gyur//de ltar rdzas kyi skye mched du//gang ltar sgoms pa de ltar 'gyur//de ni rtags chen thob pa yin//blo yi ting 'dzin rtsal rdzogs na//ngag gi bden pa'i rtsal 'byung ste//
35a8(rgyud): sreg pa'i me chen mthong ba na//zhi bar gyur cig byas pa yis//de nyid du ni zhi bar 'gyur//chu klung gnod pa'i rgyun byung na//chad par gyur cig brjod pa yis//de nyid du ni chad par 'gyur//rlung chen gnod pa'i rgyun byung na//
35b1(rgyud): zhi bar gyur cig brjod pa yis//de nyid du ni chad par 'gyur//nad pa'i tshogs ni mthong ba na//gang na na ba'i dmigs der ni//se gol gtogs pa tsam gyis kyang//nad rnams zhi bar gyur cig brjod//nad kyi tshogs rnam+sa
35b2(rgyud): zhi bar 'gyur//de bzhin dag gis brjod byas pa//thams cad ma lus grub par 'gyur//dag gi bden pa'i rtsal rdzogs na//lus kyi drod la rtsal 'byung ste//gnyid kyi rtog pa choms pa dang//lus kyi bzod pa thob pa ni/
35b3(rgyud): 'di ni dang po'i drod yin no//khams kyi zas dang bral ba dang//mdangs dang dbang po gsal 'gyur pa//'di ni gnyis pa'i drod ces bya//shing bal lta bur yang pa dang//khams bde mi bde mi 'byung zhing//nad kyi
35b4(rgyud): tshogs dang bral 'gyur ba//'di ni drod chen bstan pa yin//khams gyi zas la ma sten yang//dbang po gzi mdangs shin du gsal//'di ni ting 'dzin ro tshor brtags//gzhan yang lus kyi snga ltas ni//mnyam pa'i
35b5(rgyud): dkyil dkrung bcas nas ni//khru gang tsam du 'phar ba dang//ta la gang tsam par du 'gro//'di yang bltas chen rtags yin no//gzhan yang snang ba'i nam mkha' la//goms pas bgrong de 'gro ba dang//chu la mi
35b6(rgyud): 'byings la 'dzul//me yis mi 'tshig rlung bcing ba//'di yang snga ltas chen po yin//lus kyi bden pa'i rtsal rdzogs na//mthu yi rtsal yang 'byung ba ste//mthu yi byin rlabs bsam mi khyab//gal te dgos sam 'dod pa
35b7(rgyud): ni/sa 'am rdo 'am bye ma dag/gser dang dngul la sogs par bsgyur//skam sa dag la chu 'byin cing//sol ba dag la ljon shing 'byung//de la sogs pa'i dgos pa rnams//ji ltar 'dod pa de ltar 'grub//spyod pa'i mthu 
35b8(rgyud): dang rtsal rdzogs na//'byor pa'i yon tan rdzogs 'gyur te//yid du 'ong ba'i nor 'byung zhing//'dod pa'i bsams pa thams cad 'grub//rdzas kyi mchog tu gyur pa yi//ngo mtshar can yang rnyed par 'gyur//de dag rung ba'i snga ltas
36a1(rgyud): @#//'gro gang 'du ba'i snga ltas kyang //kun gyis bkur zhing dga' ba dang //kun gyi yid du 'ong bar 'gyur//thams cad 'dul zhing thams cad la//dbang sgyur byed pa'i mchog tu 'gyur//de dag snga ltas chen po yin//
36a2(rgyud): mtshan ma bra yi rmi ltas kyang //shin tu rgya cher 'byung ba ste//rnal 'byor can gyis shes par gyis//bag chags byang ba'i snga ltas kyang //rtag par gcer bur 'byung ba dang //khrus chen rtag tu byed pa dang //dri ma'i tshogs rnams 
36a3(rgyud): dag pa dang //chu bo dag la rgal ba dang //mtsho chu dag la rkyal pa dang //char gyi rgyun gyi 'khru pa dang //gzhan gyis bum pa gang pa'i chus//bdag gis lus la blugs pa dang //me dpung chen pos sreg pa dang //shin tu mthon pa'i rtse dag 
36a4(rgyud): las//rlung gis shin tu g.yeng zhing 'thor//'di dag bag chags byang pa'i ltas//'jigs dang bral ba'i snga ltas ni//g.yul 'gyed pa las slar log dang //nad las thar te sos pa dang //brtson ra dag las thar pa dang //bshed 
36a5(rgyud): ma'i lag nas shor ba dang //gcan zan nad nas bros pa dang //dug nad dag las sos pa dang //bcings pa dag las grol ba dang //thag pa bcad par gyur pa rnams//mtshan ma bzang po'i snga ltas yin//sa rnams thob pa'i 
36a6(rgyud): snga ltas ni//khri rkang dag las 'dzeg pa dang //phal pa'i rkas la 'dzeg pa dang //ljon shing dag la 'dzeg pa dang //gyen du shin tu 'gro ba dang //ri bo'i rtse la 'dzeg pa dang //rin chen skas la 'dzeg pa dang /mchod rten dag la 'dzeg pa 
36a7(rgyud): dang //nam mkha'i skas la 'dzeg pa rnams//'di dag don gyi snga ltas yin//sa rnams non pa'i snga ltas kyang //khri mthon steng na 'dug pa dang //glang po che la zhon pa dang //seng ge dag la zhon pa dang //khyung chen lding la zhon pa dang //
36a8(rgyud): mchod rten kha la phyin pa dang //ri bo'i rtse la 'dug pa dang //nam mkha'i dbyings na 'dug pa dang //lus las sprin phung 'byung ba dang //zla ba'i steng na 'dug pa dang /nyi ma'i steng na 'dug pa rnams//rnal 'byor sa la gnas 
36b1(rgyud): pa'i rtags//sangs rgyas sras kyi snga ltas ni//rgyal po'i bka' la brtags pa dang //bka' yi dbang por bskos pa dang //gzhan la dbang sgyur byed pa dang //'jig rten skos la 'babs pa dang //gzhan gyi sde pa dbang sdud 
36b2(rgyud): dang //yul chen dag las rgyal ba dang //bdag nyid rgyal por gyur pa dang //mgo la me tog skye ba rnams//rgyal ba'i sras su gyur pa'i ltas//chos la dbang thob snga ltas ni//glegs bam zab mo rnyed pa dang //de bzhin 'dri ba 
36b3(rgyud): mang po dang //de dag la ni dpyod pa dang //gzhan la 'chad cing ston pa dang //don nyid gtan la 'bebs pa dang //me long dag la lta ba dang //shel gyi bum pa gang ba la//lta zhing brtags te 'thung ba dang //rin chen nor bu thub pa dang //
36b4(rgyud): 'khor lo lag tu babs pa dang //nyi zla lag pas zin pa dang //ri rab klad la skor ba rnams//chos la dbang thob lnga ltas so//bden pa mthong ba'i snga ltas kyang //sngon ma mthong gi yul mthong dang //stong gsum rgya cher mthong 
36b5(rgyud): ba dang //gling rnams gzhan du phyin pa dang //ri rab kha la phyin pa dang //nam mkha' rgya cher mthong ba dang //rgya mtsho'i gting du phyin pa dang //lag na sgron mi thogs pa dang //nyi ma'i snying po blta ba dang //snang srid dpyod pa byed pa 
36b6(rgyud): rnams//chos la dbang thobaltas chen yin//lung bstan thob pa'i snga ltas ni//rol mo'i sgra rnams thos pa dang //'brug gi sgra ni grag pa dang //seng ge'i nga ro grag pa dang //chos kyi sgra yi gtam thos dang //gzugs brnyan 
36b7(rgyud): zhal ni 'dzum pa dang //sangs rgyas gsung thos sku mi mthong //lha mo glu dbyangs sgrogs pa dang //gzhal yas dag la sgra 'byung dang //grogs po grogs po zhes brjod cing //chos kyi bgro ba byed pa rnams//lung bstan 
36b8(rgyud): 'byung ba'i snga ltas so//de la sogs pa'i mtshan ma rnams//ltas chen kun du snang ba dang //gang zag las kyi dang po rnams//rig 'dzin so so'i sa pa ni//'gyur ba'i snga ltas bshad pa yin//rnal 'byor las kyi dang po la//
37a1(rgyud): @#//ltas chen 'di dag gang byung yang //'khor ba'i 'du 'dzi kun bsal nas//don la rtogs shing sa rnams sbyong //des ni don la reg par 'gyur//don la reg pa'i byin rlabs kyis//rig 'dzin rdo rjer lhun gyis 
37a2(rgyud): grub//rig 'dzin sa la gnas pa dang //sa la rtsal chen sbyong ba'i tshe//rtsal gyi cho 'phrul sna tshogs 'byung //nyi zla zhon nas 'gro ba dang //ri rab rked nas gsor ba dang //'jig rten rgyang mar 'phang ba dang //sred pa'i skal pa gzhil 
37a3(rgyud): ba la//sogs pa'i cho 'phrul thams cad kun//khyad par rtsal gyis ston par nus//de la sogs pa'i rdzu 'phrul rnams//rdzu 'phrul gyi ni cho 'phrul yin//rjes su bstan pa'i cho 'phrul ni//'dul ba'i 'gro ba thams cad la//
37a4(rgyud): so so'i skad dang chos 'thun par//'dul ba'i sgo ni sna tshogs su//so so'i skad du ston par nus//de dag rjes su bstan pa yin//chos kyi sgo rnams snang ba yi//nges pa'i cho 'phrul bstan pa ni//ting 'dzin rtsal gyi khyad par 
37a5(rgyud): gyis//'jig rten khams rnams g.yos nas kyang //thams cad ngo mtshar skye 'gyur te//sems kyi bsam pa rangs byas nas//so so dga' ba'i ro myong 'gyur//chos rnams snang ba'i cho 'phrul yin//kun nas kun du brjod 
37a6(rgyud): pa ni//gsang ba'i yi ge'i phreng ba las//rdzu 'phrul chos kyi sgra sgrogs pas//mthong yod snang grags chos rnams kun//kun kyang sngags kyi sgrar grag pas//sngags su ma gyur gang yang med//des na rtsal gyi khyad par las//
37a7(rgyud): sogs pa'i yon tan thams cad 'byung //zhes smra ba'i seng ge chen po sems dpa'i rdo rjes gsungs so////rgyud kyi rgyal po chen po rdo rje bkod pa kun 'dus rig pa'i mdo las cho 'phrul bstan pa'i le'u ste gsum pa'o//
37a8(rgyud): //de nas smra ba'i seng ge sems dpa'i rdo rje de mi 'da' ba'i dam tshig gsang ba mchog gi ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs so//de nas ting nge 'dzin de las bzhengs par gyur pa dang /'dus pa'i rnal 'byor chen po rnams 
37b1(rgyud): kyis 'di skad ces gsol to//smra ba'i seng ge sems dpa' chen po khyod ny-id ni dam tshig chen po'-i bdag nyid du bdag cag rnams kyis mthong ngo //de skad ces gsol pa dang /sems dpa' chen po des bka' stsal 
37b2(rgyud): pa/'dus pa'i rnal 'byor rnams chos thams cad kyi srog ni dam tshig yin par shes par gyis shig/de nas 'dus pa chen po'-i tshogs kyi sems dpa' chen po la 'di skad ces gsol to//sems dpa' chen po dam 
37b3(rgyud): tshig mchog gi gsang ba rnams bdag cag rnams thos par 'tshal/thos nas kyang rtogs par 'tshal/rtogs nas kyang de la gnas par 'tshal na/sems dpa' chen po rgya cher bshad du gsol//de skad ces gsol ba dang /
37b4(rgyud): sems dpa' chen pos bka' stsal pa/'dus pa chen po'-i tshogs dam tshig rnams 'di lta ste/shin tu gsang ba'-i dam tshig ni snod ma yin par gyur pa las/phyi nang gsang ba gsum dag tu//sba ba'i thabs su spyad nas kyang //
37b5(rgyud): gsang ba'-i chos rnams gsang bar bya//brtan pa'i gnas min snod ngan dag/rnam pa bzhis bsdus bye brag rnams//ma 'ongs rnal 'byor bcud can gyis//shes nas spang bar bya phyir bshad//'jig rten pa dang theg dman 
37b6(rgyud): dang //mu stegs log lta'i lta la dga'//dad med blo zhan g.yeng rmugs shing //bslab nyan mi brtson byol song mtshungs//dad log dam nyams don las gol//byang chub theg pa gting nas chad//sems kyi mu gu'i rtsa tshig pa//
37b7(rgyud): ngan 'gro'-i 'dam du kha spub yin//snod min kha spub de dag la//gsang ba smra bar bya ba min//smras na de nyid kha spub 'gyur//gsang ba'i tshig rnams khar smra yang //gab pa gsang ba'i don mi shes//skyon can spa los sgrogs rnams 
37b8(rgyud): la//gsang ba'i don rnams lhag par smra//bsnyen bsgrub gsang ba'i tshig rnams la//sgra la nyan kyang don mi gnyad//snying po'-i don gsad mi thub pa//bcud med 'khor bar zag pa yin//gsang don mi thub snod ngan la//gsang 
38a1(rgyud): @#//
38b1(rgyud): yang //ji ltar rgyu 'thun de 'drar 'gyur//skal ba dman pa'i rnal 'byor pa//rtsa nyams skyon can rang bzhin ni//ngan sems khro gtum ldan pa dang //mi zad phra men las spyod byed//gang dag mi shes dang tshul can//byis pa'i 
38b2(rgyud): rang bzhin blo zhan pas//shin tu dman pa'i lta sems kyis//tshul 'chos mkhan la bkur bar byed//gang dag dam tshig yon tan ldan//byams la gnas shing rtag tu bzod//tshul shis don gyi gzhung rnams srong //de dag la 
38b3(rgyud): ni 'dun pa med//dam tshig spre'u ltar spyod bzhin du//byang chub snying po thob pa yi//sangs rgyas rnams kyang mi 'byung ngo //'byung bar yang dag ngas ma mthong //rgyal ba'i sras gyur rnal 'byor pa//lam ston skyon sel mkhas 
38b4(rgyud): pa la//kha cig dregs dang phrag dog gis//khro spong thabs kyis smod par byed//de ni 'khor ba'i gting rdo ste//nams kyang thar pa'i khar mi lhung //mi shes pa yi rnal 'byor pa//yang dag don dang ldan pa'i mi//rjes su 'gro bar mi byed 
38b5(rgyud): cing //longs spyod 'du 'dzi gang ldan pa'i//rjes su 'gro zhing de la 'dud//de ni log pa'i lam pa yin//rnal 'byor lta dang byang chub khe//'bad thag chad par blo mi 'byung //zang zing nor dang g.yo sprul khe//chung yang cis 'grub srog dang sngo//
38b6(rgyud): rnal 'byor mkhas pa'i rang bzhin can//bum snod nang gi gsal ba ltar//shes rab mdzod kyi gter ldan yang //srog chags rkun mo'i sder mo ltar//khong nas gab sbas don ston la//blun po rnams kyis cir mi mthong //dper na 
38b7(rgyud): 'di 'drar tshul gcig ste//lus la gter 'chang mi dad ni//blun por gyur pa'i mi dag gnas//'grogs kyang gnyis mtshungs khyad med 'dra//ting 'dzin bsngags pa brjod byed cing //ting 'dzin don du gnyer ba med//yang dag 
38b8(rgyud): bsngags brjod byed cing //yang dag don du gnyer ba med//dam tshig bsngags pa rjod byed cing //dam tshig don du gnyer ba med/ji ltar 'gro gang la la dag/nor bu rin chen 'di 'dra ste//'dod pa'i yon tan kun 'byung zhes//
39a1(rgyud): @#//rin chen dag gi yon tan brjod//de la 'gro gang gzhan dag gis//de ltar 'dod pa 'byung gyur na//de ltar cung zad yod dam zhes//dris kyang med pas slar smras pa//gang zhig dgos pa skong ba yi//yon tan cung 
39a2(rgyud): zad smos par zad//de nyid bdag la med ces smra//de bzhin rnal 'byor mi brtson pas//bslab par bya ba'i chos bral la//chos nyid bsngags pa rjod byed kyang //de dag la ni chos nyid med//de las spyod pa'i log spyod rnams//
39a3(rgyud): dam tshig can gyis spang bar bya//rnal 'byor chos 'dzin gang dag gis//rnal 'byor nyams 'gyur log sred can//gsang ba 'jigs pa'i dgrar mthong nas//de dag sdig pa mthong ba ltar//skrod cing chad pas gcod byed pa//
39a4(rgyud): skyes bu de ni rgyal ba'i sras//dper na blon po gang dag gis//nyes byas khrims dang 'gal 'gyur na//'gal bar mi gtong khrims dang sbyor//bka' yi yo srong blon po de//rgyal po'i thugs 'dzin dam pa zhes//kun du rgya cher 
39a5(rgyud): grags par 'gyur//ldum rar dug gi sdong skyes na//bdag pos dug du shes ma thag/rtsad nas phyung ste med par byed//ci phyir ldum zas de dag las//dug 'phel gyur na 'chi phyir ro//de bzhin nyams 'gyur dug can de//
39a6(rgyud): brtul zhugs can gyi rnal 'byor des//gnas sam lo yis spyugs te spang //gang dag mi spong mgon byed pa//de dag gsang ba'i dam sri yin//don la rnal 'byor gang zhig gis//tha tshigs zab mor bcas rnams las//
39a7(rgyud): bsrung ba'i bsam pa mi g.yo bzhin//rtsa ba yan lag bcas rnams la//gal te ma tshor zhig gyur kyang //rnam smin yod kyang rtsa mi nyams//ji ltar glang po a ne ya//shi nas ro ru gyur mod kyang //'ge'u wang rtsis ni don byed 
39a8(rgyud): ltar//rnal 'byor nyams de ror gyur yang //gzhan gyis bkur ba'i gnas yin zhing //de yi don byed nus pa yin//de ltar mod kyang 'di kun rnams//rmi lam du yang mthong mi bya//ci phyir 'o ma nyung du zhig/rul bas 'o ma kun 
39b1(rgyud): rul bzhin//rnal 'byor nyams gyur gcig gis ni//rnal 'byor can kun ma rung 'gyur//'khor ba'i g.yul rgyal byed pa yi//log pa'i rnal 'byor thams cad kun//don la mkhas pa'i rnal 'byor pas//thams cad bsgrims 
39b2(rgyud): te spang bar bya//gal te ma spangs log spyad na//'khor ba'i g.yul ni rgyal 'gyur te//bden pa'i byin rlabs mthu chung 'gyur//kun kyang dge sbyong don chung zhes//yang dag don las yid byung zhing //log pa'i chos la spro bar byed//
39b3(rgyud): kun kyang log pa'i las spyad pas//yid du mi 'ong sna tshogs 'byung //des ni sgro skur gnyis su 'gyur//bka' yi chad pa gang yin pa//ye shes spyan ltar rang bzhin gyis//nams kyang gnod pa yod mi mdzad//de yi 'khor 
39b4(rgyud): na gnas pa yi//gang dag ma rungs mthu chen rnams//rnal 'byor log par spyod rnams kyi//skyon mthong tsam na chad pas gcod//thams cad ngan 'gror spyi'u tshugs ltung //sgrub pa'i snod mchog gcig yod de//'di 
39b5(rgyud): dang mi mthun kun rdzogs pa'i//rgyud ma nyan don ldan ngang tshul bzang //ci la chags med gtong ldan sems//shes rab rnam grol lde mig gis//gti mug tha ram blo che nas//chos kyi snying po'i mig ldan pas//rtag chad 
39b6(rgyud): phrad mthong dbu ma'i yul//ma nor lam du 'jug shes pas//sdong po sdong chen rab dul ba'o//tha tshig lung las byung ba bzhin//gzhung srong thub par byed pa de//rgyal ba'i sras po yin pas na//de la gsang sgo dbye bar 
39b7(rgyud): bya//ji ltar dam tshig thub pa ltar//de bzhin yid bzhin nor bu ste//yid 'ong 'dod dang bde ba 'byung //rigs 'dzin rgyal ba kun yum mo//de bzhin dam tshig thub pa ltar/gnas par byed pa'i rten yang yin//dam 
39b8(rgyud): tshig kun gyi srog yin te//dam tshig kun gyi nor mchog go/dam tshig 'di ni 'jig pa'i skyabs//dam tshig bgrod pa lam gyi mchog/dam tshig chu bo sgrol ba'i gru//dam tshig dngos grub dam pa yin//dam tshig 
40a1(rgyud): grogs kyi mchog yin te//dam tshig long ba'i mig bu yin//ji ltar dam tshig thub pa ltar//gzhan gyi sde ba 'joms pa yin//'khor ba'i g.yul las rgyal bar byed//des na dam tshig rgyun bzhin srung //mdor na 
40a2(rgyud): bdag gi sgo gsum po//dag pa gsum gyi mtha' bzung nas//lha dang grogs dang slob dpon dang //bdag nyid dag pa ma khrel ba'i//tshul gsum mi gnod dam bcas gsum//yongs su ma nyams mdo bzhi po//mthar phyin rgya 
40a3(rgyud): rtags rnam bzhir bcas//mdo don dran pa la bgrang bsngo//brtul zhugs spyod pa'i dam tshig ni//dam tshig kun gyi thog mar 'gro//wa dang cang shes gyi ling dang //blang dor ming bral khro chen bdag/shin du glang chen mos 'dri 
40a4(rgyud): sems//dpa' brtul stag mchog rdam zhing gtum//mkha' lding khyung chen lhag mi lta//gtum thung don chen sdong gcig pa//yid thub mi ldog rgya mtsho'i gser//gti mug lkug pa'i blo tshol ldan//ri bo mi 'gyur brtan pa 
40a5(rgyud): che//mkha' ltar yangs dog kun dang bral//gnam lcags lce yi drag shul can//phyag na rdo rje'i brtul zhugs bdag/zon bag khu 'phrig kha rog sems//nges pa'i nga rgyal glang tshul ldan//zla bral seng ge ri dgas rgyal//
40a6(rgyud): re 'jam bya ltar chags 'grogs blo//g.yo ldan lcag chen sgyu ma'i rgya//gtsang sme med par kun spyod cing //thogs med gsod sbyong lce spyang khwa//che ba'i nga rgyal stag mchog blo//kun 'dzam blo ldan nam mkha' lding //sa thub bag 
40a7(rgyud): len rgyal 'khor skyong //rnal 'byor brtul zhugs chen po ni//bcu gnyis dang ni gsum blor ldan//goms nas nyams su len byed pa//rnal 'byor gsang ba'i bka' ba yin//dal ba'i rgyud ldan tha tshig can//sku dang gsung 
40a8(rgyud): thugs rdo rje yi//gsung dang brjod dang gsang ba dang //yan lag spyad pa'i dam tshig dang //yid 'ong dang du blang ba dang //brtul zhugs zab mor spyad pa dang //rnal 'byor rnal 'byor thams cad kyi//mi 'dor mi spong sbyar ba yi//
40b1(rgyud): rig ldan shes par bya ba dang //thob cing grub par bya ba yin//re re la yang lnga lnga ste//rtsa ba dang ni yan lag las//bcu gnyis dang ni brgyad ldan pa//spyad par bya ba'i nor mchog yin//rnal 'byor byang chub sems rgyud 
40b2(rgyud): la//nges par brtags te spyod pa'i bu//zab mor rdzogs mchog bde ba yi//blo la nges pa'i lung 'di stan//'di ni sngar 'gro tha tshig ste//zab mo sgrub pa'i sngon logs su//rdo rje tha tshig drag po'o//gcan 'phrang lam rnams bsrung 
40b3(rgyud): ba'i phyir//spyi dang khyad par dam tshig rnams//nges pa'i don gyi bsrung byas na//de ni dbyig gi gnod chen yin//de ltar spyi yi sdom pa la//gnas nas dus kyi skabs 'di ru//bsrung bya thub par bya ba ni//mi shigs pa ni sang 
40b4(rgyud): ge ste//gcan zan rgyal po yin pas so//za ma tog ni 'dod pa'i gter//dug spyo pa ni der mi rigs//rin chen myu gu'i lcug phran dag/de ni breg pa'i rigs ma yin//gangs gi zhun chu khol ma dag/mkhas pas btung bar 
40b5(rgyud): bya ba min//pad ma ze'u 'bru kha ma bye//zla zer ma yin kha mi dbye//ma brtags bug rdol can gyi snod//kha mi dbye zhing bcud mi blug/don dang mi ldan bcos mi brtags//bsrel zhing spyad par bya ba min//shel dkar rin chen 
40b6(rgyud): sgong dag/'dam rdzab khung du bsub mi bya//seng ge dkar mo'i 'o ma dag/dug can snod du blugs pa min//yid bzhin nor bu rin po che//bsreg par bya ba yod ma yin//khyung chen gshog pa mkhar brkyang pa//de dag gcag 
40b7(rgyud): par bya ba min//gnam lcags me yi dur mo dag/brdab par bya ba ma yin no//stag gzig 'drol bas bas byas pa'i//las kyi lhag ma sdu mi bya//rdo rje brag chen gtams pa'i gzhi//de dag gzhig par bya ba min//rdo rje ra ba'i go 
40b8(rgyud): tshags ni//bsdam bya bral bar bya ba min//bud shing sreg pa'i sgron ma dag/g.yeng ba'i rlung gis bskyod mi bya//rdo rje tshig gi chu rgyun dag/'ol kar gcad par bya ba min//rgyal po bka' rtags phyag rgya dag/nam zhar dbyi 
41a1(rgyud): @#//
41b1(rgyud): phog par 'gyur//de dag bcas kar 'gyur ba'i dgra//lha dman spyod rtsing 'chol ba yis//bka' ye gzhung bor log par spyad//nor pas bar chad 'byung ba'i dgra//dam gtsor spyod pa'i las rnams yin//'da' dka' 
41b2(rgyud): rdo rje mchog gsum po//btang snyoms phyal bar shor ba ni//rtsa ba rul pa'i ljon shing bzhin//bka' chad chen po 'bab pa'i gnas//brtsam pa las la sbyor ba'i tshe//'bad pa'i rtsal shugs tshungs pa yis//don rnams grub 
41b3(rgyud): pa ma yin te//'bras bur mi 'gyur 'phrog pa'i dgra//rnal 'byor chen por spyod pa la//'dod pa'i nor mchog 'phrog pa'i dgra//thabs la mkhas pa'i skyes bu yis//skyon gyi dgra bzhi spang bar bya//srid gsum 
41b4(rgyud): bsgrol pa'i zhing 'di dag/sangs rgyas zhing du rtogs pa ni//sa rnams gcig car sbyangs pa ste//zhing rnams dag pa'i mchog yin no//khams gsum mchod pa'i rgyu 'di dag/yo byad chen po'i mchog yin te//rgyal 
41b5(rgyud): ba thams cad mnyes pa'i rgyu//rmad du byung ba'i mchod pa'o//'khor ba dur khrod gnas 'di dag/bde ba chen po'i gnas yin te//ma nor ma bcos chos kyi dbyings//brtsal med lhun gyis grub pa la//dug lnga rgyu ba'i 
41b6(rgyud): rtsa ba dag/ye shes lnga yi rtsa ba ste//kun shes kun rigs gsal ba'o//rang rig ye shes rang las byung //rnal 'byor chos mig ldan pa yis//nges pa'i don du 'di ltar blta'o//'di ni rgyun gyi dam tshig ste//brjod du med pa 
41b7(rgyud): bsrung ba'o//bsrung ba thub par bya ba la//dam tshig rtsal gyi khyad par ni//gang dag rig pa 'dzin pa yi//blo la nges pa'i lung du bstan//'dod pas chags dang 'joms pa dang //gti mug nga rgyal phrag dog rnams//rnal 'byor 
41b8(rgyud): chen po'i dam tshig ste//don dang ldan pa'i skyes bus spyad//sbyor dang grol bar byed pa dang //ma byin len dang ngag sprul byed//rnal 'byor dam tshig spyod pa'i mchog/spyad par bya ba'i las yin no//rgyal ba sems can thams cad 
42a1(rgyud): @#//dang //dngos por brtags pa thams cad kyang //don gyi ngang du gcig pa ste//de nyid sku gsung thugs kyi bdag/mtshan ma'i chos rnams thams cad ni//mnyam pa chen po'i ngang du gsal//theg pa 'og ma'i khyad par 
42a2(rgyud): rnams//kun kyang rang rig blo la gsal//yon tan ci 'byung mi brjod cing //'og ma rnams la mi smod cing //thams cad mnyam pa'i dam tshig ste//'di ltar shes na tshogs mchog rdzogs//gzugs rnams rnam par snang ba mdzad//
42a3(rgyud): tshig rnams brjod pa snang mtha' yas//rnam rtog kun tu sems rdo rje//rig dang tshor ba rin chen 'byung //yid g.yo byed pa don kun rdzogs//stan 'gan rang bzhin spyod pa'i gnas//zhing bcu dag dang ldan pa na//bde ba chen po'i 
42a4(rgyud): longs spyod de//thogs med phrin las rnam bzhir rdzogs//phyi yi yul dang nang gi sems//thams cad mnyam pa nyid du gnas//de la sgrib dang mi sgrib ces//mnyam pa'i rgyal pos yod ma gsungs//thams cad mnyam 
42a5(rgyud): nyid dbyings gyur pas//phung po lnga don ye shes lnga//sku gsung thugs kyi dbang phyug ni//phyag rgya chen po'i rigs 'dzin yin//yul dang ye shes rang bzhin las//gzhan gyi rang bzhin med pas na//mtshan ma'i rnam pa 
42a6(rgyud): cir snang yang //de la chad pa'i gnas med do//dam tshig dam pa gang zhe na//rtog pa dam pa dam tshig ste//rtogs par byed pa gang yin pa//de ni dam tshig chen po'o//thams cad mnyam pa'i ngang las gnas//ting nge 'dzin gyi 
42a7(rgyud): cho 'phrul las//dngos su spyad pa thams cad bya//de ni dam tshig chen po yin//snod du gyur pa yum gyi dbyings//bcud rnams rgyal ba yab kyi mkha'//'byung 'jug rig pas longs spyod de//'di ni rdo rje tha tshig yin//srid 
42a8(rgyud): pa'i sems can thams cad dang //thar pa chos kyi rnam grangs kun//so sor thams cad snang ba ste//kun kyang mnyam pa'i ngang du gsal//spyod pa'i las dang rtog pa dang //mtshan mar gyur pa thams cad kyang //sems kyi phyi 
42b1(rgyud): nang spyod yul de//brtags na longs spyod tshogs su 'gyur//ma skyes stong pa sku yi rgya//rang bzhin smon med gsung gi don//mtshan med yid las 'das pa thugs//rnam gsum bde chen sangs rgyas so//mnyam pa'i lta bas 
42b2(rgyud): dbang sgyur na//'khor ba thams cad dbyings la snang //las kun rdzogs pa'i mchog yin te//gzhan du 'phrin las lus pa med//sdug bsngal byang chub chen po ste//'khor ba ye nas mya ngan 'das//sgrib pa rnam dag chos kyi 
42b3(rgyud): dbyings//nyon mongs kun kyang byang chub don//snang ba med pa'i don mthong dang //sa dang sa yi rim par ni//'di dang 'di zhes gzung du med//'di na snang med thob pa'i gnas//sku dang gsung thugs mi g.yo ba'i//ji bzhin nyid kyi 
42b4(rgyud): ngang la gnas//ji ltar bsgyur yang chos nyid pas//chad pa'i gnas zhes brjod dang bral//med dang phyal ba gcig po dang //lhun gyis rdzogs pa'i dam tshig che//'di zhes ris su chad med pa//'di ni bsrung pa'i dam tshig go//
42b5(rgyud): spyad dang spyod par bya med pas//srid dang mu stegs 'jig rten pa'i//spyod par snang ba thams cad kun//lhun gyis grub pa'i mdzad spyod do//ma spangs lhun grub dbyer med pas//dbyings las gzhan na lta yul med//'di ni 
42b6(rgyud): lta ba'i dam tshig go//sa chu me rlung snod bcud bcas//kun kyang rang bzhin chos nyid do//chos nyid don la gzhan med pa//rang byung ye shes gsal ba'o//yid bzhin nor bu rin po che//ma tsal rang la yod pa yin//'dod pa 
42b7(rgyud): 'byung ba'i longs spyod de//bde chen longs spyod tshogs yin no//ngan song mtho ris thams cad dang //'phags lam 'khor ba'i chos rnams kun//rang bzhin so so'i mi dmigs te//sangs rgyas kyi ni rang bzhin yin//
42b8(rgyud): spyod dang gzugs rigs thams cad kun//phyag rgya chen po'i rang bzhin te//sku gsung thugs tshig rgya cher ston//don rig thugs kyi rang bzhin no//ma skyes pa dang ma 'byung ba//mi gnas pa la chags pa med//don gyi 
43a1(rgyud): @#//khyad par rtogs chud na//kun du bzang po mchog gi sa'o//mtshan dang dpe byad kun rdzogs te//thams cad sku gsung thugs kyi don//bdag nyid chen por thams cad rdzogs//lhun rdzogs bde ba chen po yi//

53a3(rgyud): dman med par thob//'di ni tshigs med don po che//thabs la mkhas pa'i rigs 'dzin yin//de bzhin spyod pa gzhan dag la//dga' bas rjes su yi rang gis//dge ba spyad dang ma spyad gnyis//'bras bu mnyam por thob

53a7(rgyud): bsam yas la//sbyin pa'i tshogs chen spel pas ni//'bum gyi char yang mi phod do//ci phyir zhe na gcig mnyes pas//rgyal ba thams cad mnyes pa yin//nam mkha' khyab pa'i rgyal ba dang //nyag cig snang ba'i rgyal ba gnyis//

53b2(rgyud): tshogs la spyod pa'i gsal ldan ni//'di tshe gcig la mngon sangs rgyas//sbyin pa spel ba'i dge ldan ni//bskal pa grangs med gsum gyis grub//de bas gnas 'dir spyod pa dag/rnal 'byor gsal ldan tshul 
53b3(rgyud): bzhin spyod//'di dag rtag tu su 'chang ba//rdo rje sems dpa' la sogs pa'i//che yon rtag tu ci 'dra bar//don gyi gzugs dang bcas par 'thob//de yang ji lta bu zhe na//khyim bdag mes byin bya ba de//tshom bu rgya dang 
53b4(rgyud): bcas pa zhig/rgyun gyi rtag par 'di spyad pas//sangs rgyas rdo rje sems dpa' ni//'khor dang bcas pa ci 'dra bar//skye ba de yis nges par thob//sku dang gsung thugs rdo rje yi//don du nges par sgrub pa'i tshogs//rtogs 
53b5(rgyud): nas smra ba'i gang byed pa//goms dang brjod dang smos pa yis//chos kyi rgyal por de 'gyur zhing //yon tan spyi gtsug nyid du 'gyur//de yang ji lta bu zhe na //drang srong rnal 'byor ka la ya nas//drang srong rnam pa mang po 
53b6(rgyud): la|tshogs 'di yon tan rgya cher grags//don rnams nges par bshad pa las//drang srong mang pos rig 'dzin thob//ston par byed pa'i ka la yan//gsang bdag chos kyi rgyal por gyur//'on tang gsang ba'i don 'di dag/

55a7(rgyud): rje zhe sems khyod la 'dud//gsang ba yang gsang khyod la 'dud//gsang pa ye gsang khyod la 'dud/dkyil 'khor rgyal po khyod la 'dud//'khor lo snying po khyod la 'dud//gsang chen snying po tshul ci lags//bka'

65b8(rgyud): du yongs su rdzogs par mthong ngo //dbu las byung ba'i sangs rgyas nyi shu rtsa gcig dang /rgyal mtshan chen po bcu la rdzogs pa'i rnal 'byor yongs su rdzogs par mthong/dbu'i gtsug tor rgyal mtshan rtse mo la gser gyi

66b2(rgyud): nyid rgyun gyi ting nge 'dzin zhesabya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs so//chos kyi rgyun gyi ting nge 'dzin 'di gsungs so//nam mkha' don gyi glegs bam la//skar tshogs rig pa'i yig bris te//'byung chen bzhi yi sgra sgrags 
66b3(rgyud): pas//gsang sngags chos kyi rgyun mi 'chad//chos kyi rgyun gyi ting nges 'dzin zhes bya ba yongs su rdzogs pa'o//de nas yang bcom ldan 'da'as gsang ba mchog gi bdagapo des bdud rtsi rgyun kyi ting nge 'dzin la snyoms 
66b4(rgyud): par zhugs te/'di skad ces gsungs so//stong gsum ban da'i bzang khong du//srid gsum sems can dug chen las//dus gsum sangs rgyas rtsi sbyar bas//bdud rtsi dkyil 'khor lhun gyis rdzogs zhes gsungs so//

67a1(rgyud): @#//ces gsungs so//de nas yang sems dpa'i rdo rje snying po de sgrol ba rgyun gyi ting nge 'dzin zhes bya ba la snyoms par zhugs te/'di skad gsungs so//'khor ba bsgral ba'i zhing 'di dag/ye shes 'bar ba'i mtshon 
67a2(rgyud): chen gyis//rang bzhin med par bsgral nas ni//mnyam nyid chos sku chen por gton//zhes gsungs so//de nas yar sems dpa'i rdo rje snying bo de sku gdung rgyun gyi ting nge 'dzin zhe bya ba la snyoms par zhug te/'di skad 
67a3(rgyud): ces gsungs so//'dod khams rmang gi ri rab la//gzugs khams pa da rim khri 'phang brtan//gzugs med bum rten bum par bcas//gdung chen chos skur rgyun du gnas zhes gsungs so//de nas kyang sems dpa'i
